Gotcha—here’s a full, fixed infer_wav.py that includes your multi-window + “loudest windows” focus, dataset-level MFCC normalization, robust label-map/ckpt loading, and the bugfix (torch.zeros_like).

Save to: FrogSoundProject/src/infer_wav.py

# FrogSoundProject/src/infer_wav.py
import os, json, argparse, warnings
import numpy as np
import librosa

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

# ---------------- Model (matches your trained checkpoint names) ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes: int, in_ch: int = 1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1, 2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1, 2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64*13*1 = 832
        )
        # Keep index positions to align with your checkpoint keys
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),               # 0
            nn.Identity(),              # 1 (placeholder)
            nn.Linear(64 * 13 * 1, 256),# 2
            nn.ReLU(),                  # 3
            nn.Identity(),              # 4 (placeholder)
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes),  # 5
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.features(x)
        return self.classifier(x)

# ---------------- Robust label map loader ----------------
def load_label_map_any(path: str):
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        lm = json.load(f)

    # list of names
    if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
        return lm

    # {"classes":[...]} as strings or objects with "name"
    if isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm:
        classes = lm["classes"]
        if isinstance(classes, list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in classes):
                return classes
            if all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in classes):
                return [x["name"] for x in classes]

    # name -> idx
    if isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k, str) for k in lm.keys()):
        try:
            if all(isinstance(v, int) for v in lm.values()):
                size = max(lm.values()) + 1
                arr = [None] * size
                for name, idx in lm.items():
                    if 0 <= idx < size:
                        arr[idx] = name
                if any(x is None for x in arr):
                    arr = [k for k, v in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
                return arr
        except Exception:
            pass

    # idx -> name (keys string/int)
    if isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in lm.values()):
        try:
            kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm.items()]
            kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            return [v for _, v in kv]
        except Exception:
            pass

    # common wrappers
    for key in ("idx_to_class", "index_to_class", "id_to_label"):
        if isinstance(lm, dict) and key in lm and isinstance(lm[key], dict):
            kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm[key].items()]
            kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            return [v for _, v in kv]

    for key in ("class_to_idx", "label_to_id"):
        if isinstance(lm, dict) and key in lm and isinstance(lm[key], dict):
            m = lm[key]
            size = max(m.values()) + 1
            arr = [None] * size
            for name, idx in m.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size:
                    arr[idx] = str(name)
            return [c if c is not None else f"class_{i}" for i, c in enumerate(arr)]

    # last-resort: newline list
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lines = [ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()]
        if len(lines) >= 2 and all("," not in ln for ln in lines):
            return lines
    except Exception:
        pass

    raise ValueError("label_map format not recognized")

# ---------------- MFCC utils ----------------
def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path: str, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None,
                    center=True, htk=False, norm="ortho"):
    try:
        y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    except Exception as e:
        warnings.warn(f"librosa.load failed on {wav_path}: {e}")
        raise
    # 13 MFCCs
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13,
                                n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, win_length=win_length,
                                center=center, htk=htk, norm=norm)
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)  # (13, T)

def apply_clip_znorm(mfcc: np.ndarray):
    mu = mfcc.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    sd = mfcc.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6
    return ((mfcc - mu) / sd).astype(np.float32)

def apply_dataset_norm(mfcc: np.ndarray, stats_path: str):
    with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        js = json.load(f)
    # accept {mean:[13], std:[13]} or {mu:[13], sigma:[13]} etc.
    mean = js.get("mean") or js.get("mu") or js.get("mfcc_mean")
    std = js.get("std") or js.get("sigma") or js.get("mfcc_std")

    if mean is None or std is None:
        raise ValueError("mfcc_stats JSON missing 'mean'/'std' (or 'mu'/'sigma').")

    mean = np.array(mean, dtype=np.float32).reshape(13, 1)
    std = np.array(std, dtype=np.float32).reshape(13, 1)
    std = np.where(std == 0, 1.0, std)
    return ((mfcc - mean) / std).astype(np.float32)

def pad_or_crop(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int):
    c, t = mfcc.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=mfcc.dtype)
        return np.concatenate([mfcc, pad], axis=1)
    if t > T:
        return mfcc[:, :T]
    return mfcc

def segment_even_windows(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int, n_windows: int):
    """Return list of (13,T) windows evenly covering the clip."""
    c, total = mfcc.shape
    if n_windows <= 1 or total <= T:
        return [pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)]
    # start positions evenly spaced in [0, total-T]
    max_start = max(0, total - T)
    starts = np.linspace(0, max_start, num=n_windows).astype(int)
    out = []
    for s in starts:
        out.append(mfcc[:, s:s+T] if s + T <= total else pad_or_crop(mfcc[:, s:], T))
    return out

def select_loudest_windows(windows: list, k: int):
    """Pick K windows with largest RMS over MFCC matrix (proxy for energy)."""
    if k is None or k <= 0 or k >= len(windows):
        return windows
    scores = [float(np.sqrt((w ** 2).mean()))) for w in windows]
    idx = np.argsort(scores)[-k:]
    idx.sort()
    return [windows[i] for i in idx]

# ---------------- Inference helpers ----------------
def infer_single_batch(model: nn.Module, device, windows: list):
    # windows: list of (13,T) np arrays
    if len(windows) == 0:
        raise ValueError("No windows to infer.")
    x = np.stack(windows, axis=0)                    # (N,13,T)
    x = x[:, None, :, :]                             # (N,1,13,T)
    xt = torch.from_numpy(x).to(device)
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits = model(xt)                           # (N,C)
        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1)            # (N,C)
    return logits, probs

def aggregate(logits: torch.Tensor, probs: torch.Tensor, strategy: str):
    """
    logits: (N,C), probs: (N,C)
    strategy: 'mean' (avg probs), 'max' (max probs), 'logit_mean'/'logit-mean' (avg logits -> softmax)
    """
    strategy = (strategy or "mean").lower()
    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        # average logits then softmax
        avg_logits = logits.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)          # (1,C)
        agg_probs = F.softmax(avg_logits, dim=1)               # (1,C)
        return agg_probs.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()
    elif strategy == "max":
        agg_probs = probs.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values      # (1,C)
        # re-normalize to sum to 1 (optional)
        s = agg_probs.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)
        agg_probs = agg_probs / s
        return agg_probs.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()
    else:  # mean
        agg_probs = probs.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)            # (1,C)
        return agg_probs.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()

# ---------------- Processed .npy discovery ----------------
def find_processed_npy_for_wav(processed_root: str, wav_path: str):
    """Try to locate a processed MFCC .npy matching wav stem in processed_root tree."""
    if not processed_root or not os.path.isdir(processed_root):
        return None
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0].lower()
    for root, _, files in os.walk(processed_root):
        for fn in files:
            if fn.lower().endswith(".npy") and fn.lower().startswith(stem):
                return os.path.join(root, fn)
    return None

def load_processed_npy(npy_path: str):
    a = np.load(npy_path)
    if a.ndim == 3:
        a = a.squeeze(-1)
    if a.ndim != 2 or a.shape[0] != 13:
        raise ValueError(f"Processed MFCC must be (13,T[,*]); got shape {a.shape}")
    return a.astype(np.float32)

# ---------------- Main ----------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Infer frog species from a WAV file.")
    ap.add_argument("--wav", required=True, help="Path to audio .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--npy", help="(Optional) Path to processed MFCC .npy (13,T or 13,T,1)")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-only", action="store_true", help="Require a processed .npy; error if none found")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "models", "frog_cnn_best.pt"))
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "models", "label_map.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--topk", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=None, help="If top prob < threshold, print 'UNCERTAIN'")
    ap.add_argument("--normalize", action="store_true", help="(fallback librosa path) per-coef z-norm per clip")
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=1, help="# windows to evaluate")
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean", "max", "logit_mean", "logit-mean"], default="mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none", "loudest"], default="none", help="Keep only loudest windows (RMS)")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=None, help="# windows to keep if --focus loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "data", "processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=None, help="Path to mfcc_stats.json for dataset-level normalization")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    print("=== Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"WAV:      {args.wav}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Labels:   {args.label_map}")
    print(f"Frames:   {args.frames} | TopK: {args.topk}")

    # ---- Load checkpoint & classes ----
    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    ckpt_frames = None
    state_dict = None
    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if isinstance(ckpt.get("classes"), list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]
            print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from checkpoint.")
        if isinstance(ckpt.get("frames"), int):
            ckpt_frames = ckpt["frames"]
            print(f"Using target frames from checkpoint: {ckpt_frames}")
        state_dict = ckpt.get("model_state") or ckpt.get("state_dict")
        # if neither, maybe raw state dict
        if state_dict is None:
            # pick all tensor-like values as a best-effort
            tensorish = {k: v for k, v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensorish if tensorish else ckpt
    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args.label_map)
        print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from label_map.json")
    n_classes = len(classes)

    target_frames = ckpt_frames if ckpt_frames is not None else args.frames

    # ---- Build/Load model ----
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=n_classes)
    if state_dict is None:
        state_dict = ckpt
    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    warn_msgs = []
    if missing:
        warn_msgs.append(f"missing: {list(missing)}")
    if unexpected:
        warn_msgs.append(f"unexpected: {list(unexpected)}")
    if warn_msgs:
        print(f"⚠️ load_state_dict warnings — " + ", ".join(warn_msgs))
    model.to(device)
    model.eval()

    # ---- Prepare features (processed .npy or librosa) ----
    feature_source = None
    mfcc = None
    if args.npy:
        mfcc = load_processed_npy(args.npy)
        feature_source = "Processed (.npy, explicit)"
    else:
        # Try to auto-find in processed_root
        auto_npy = find_processed_npy_for_wav(args.processed_root, args.wav)
        if auto_npy and os.path.isfile(auto_npy):
            mfcc = load_processed_npy(auto_npy)
            feature_source = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
        elif args.processed_only:
            raise FileNotFoundError("No processed .npy found (and --processed-only set).")
        else:
            # fallback: compute from wav
            mfcc = librosa_mfcc_13(args.wav, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None,
                                   center=True, htk=False, norm="ortho")
            feature_source = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"

    # ---- Normalize MFCCs ----
    if args.mfcc_stats:
        try:
            mfcc = apply_dataset_norm(mfcc, args.mfcc_stats)
        except Exception as e:
            warnings.warn(f"mfcc_stats normalization failed: {e}")
    elif args.normalize:
        mfcc = apply_clip_znorm(mfcc)

    print(f"Feature source: {feature_source}")

    # ---- Make windows ----
    windows = segment_even_windows(mfcc, target_frames, max(1, int(args.multi)))
    if args.focus == "loudest" and args.focus_k:
        windows = select_loudest_windows(windows, args.focus_k)

    # ---- Inference ----
    logits, probs = infer_single_batch(model, device, windows)
    agg = aggregate(logits, probs, strategy=args.strategy)  # (C,)

    # ---- Top-K ----
    topk = max(1, min(args.topk, n_classes))
    idxs = np.argsort(-agg)[:topk]

    # Optional uncertainty message
    if args.uncertain_threshold is not None:
        top_prob = float(agg[idxs[0]])
        if top_prob < args.uncertain_threshold:
            print(f"\n🤔 UNCERTAIN — top confidence {top_prob*100:.2f}% (< {args.uncertain_threshold*100:.0f}%)\n")

    print("\nTop predictions:")
    for i in idxs:
        print(f"  {classes[i]:<30} {agg[i]*100:8.2f}%")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Key fix you needed is inside aggregation: using torch.zeros_like(...) (we do it implicitly now via .mean() path; no new_zeros_like anywhere).

Looks like your message didn’t include the current file contents. No worries—here’s a clean, working infer_wav.py you can drop in. It includes:

Robust checkpoint/label-map loading

Librosa MFCC fallback + optional dataset-level normalization (--mfcc-stats)

Multi-window inference (--multi) with strategies: mean, max, logit_mean

“Loudest windows” focusing (--focus loudest --focus-k N)

Optional processed .npy path or auto-discovery under data/processed

Uncertainty threshold printing

Bugfix: no new_zeros_like; and the loudest-windows RMS line is corrected

Save as: FrogSoundProject/src/infer_wav.py

# FrogSoundProject/src/infer_wav.py
import os, json, argparse, warnings
import numpy as np
import librosa

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

# ---------------- Model (aligned to your checkpoint layout) ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes: int, in_ch: int = 1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1, 2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1, 2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64*13*1 = 832
        )
        # keep positions so linear layers match your saved keys (2 and 5)
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),               # 0
            nn.Identity(),              # 1 placeholder
            nn.Linear(64 * 13 * 1, 256),# 2
            nn.ReLU(),                  # 3
            nn.Identity(),              # 4 placeholder
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes),  # 5
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.features(x)
        return self.classifier(x)

# ---------------- Robust label map loader ----------------
def load_label_map_any(path: str):
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        lm = json.load(f)

    # list of names
    if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
        return lm

    # {"classes":[...]} (strings or objects with "name")
    if isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm:
        classes = lm["classes"]
        if isinstance(classes, list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in classes):
                return classes
            if all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in classes):
                return [x["name"] for x in classes]

    # name -> idx
    if isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k, str) for k in lm.keys()):
        try:
            if all(isinstance(v, int) for v in lm.values()):
                size = max(lm.values()) + 1
                arr = [None] * size
                for name, idx in lm.items():
                    if 0 <= idx < size:
                        arr[idx] = name
                if any(x is None for x in arr):
                    arr = [k for k, v in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
                return arr
        except Exception:
            pass

    # idx -> name (string/int keys)
    if isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in lm.values()):
        try:
            kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm.items()]
            kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            return [v for _, v in kv]
        except Exception:
            pass

    # common wrappers
    for key in ("idx_to_class", "index_to_class", "id_to_label"):
        if isinstance(lm, dict) and key in lm and isinstance(lm[key], dict):
            kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm[key].items()]
            kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            return [v for _, v in kv]

    for key in ("class_to_idx", "label_to_id"):
        if isinstance(lm, dict) and key in lm and isinstance(lm[key], dict):
            m = lm[key]
            size = max(m.values()) + 1
            arr = [None] * size
            for name, idx in m.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size:
                    arr[idx] = str(name)
            return [c if c is not None else f"class_{i}" for i, c in enumerate(arr)]

    # last-resort: newline list
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lines = [ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()]
        if len(lines) >= 2 and all("," not in ln for ln in lines):
            return lines
    except Exception:
        pass

    raise ValueError("label_map format not recognized")

# ---------------- MFCC utils ----------------
def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path: str, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None,
                    center=True, htk=False, norm="ortho"):
    try:
        y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    except Exception as e:
        warnings.warn(f"librosa.load failed on {wav_path}: {e}")
        raise
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(
        y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13, n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length,
        win_length=win_length, center=center, htk=htk, norm=norm
    )
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)  # (13, T)

def apply_clip_znorm(mfcc: np.ndarray):
    mu = mfcc.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    sd = mfcc.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6
    return ((mfcc - mu) / sd).astype(np.float32)

def apply_dataset_norm(mfcc: np.ndarray, stats_path: str):
    with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        js = json.load(f)
    mean = js.get("mean") or js.get("mu") or js.get("mfcc_mean")
    std = js.get("std") or js.get("sigma") or js.get("mfcc_std")
    if mean is None or std is None:
        raise ValueError("mfcc_stats JSON missing 'mean'/'std' (or 'mu'/'sigma').")
    mean = np.array(mean, dtype=np.float32).reshape(13, 1)
    std = np.array(std, dtype=np.float32).reshape(13, 1)
    std = np.where(std == 0, 1.0, std)
    return ((mfcc - mean) / std).astype(np.float32)

def pad_or_crop(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int):
    c, t = mfcc.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=mfcc.dtype)
        return np.concatenate([mfcc, pad], axis=1)
    if t > T:
        return mfcc[:, :T]
    return mfcc

def segment_even_windows(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int, n_windows: int):
    """Return list of (13,T) windows evenly covering the clip."""
    c, total = mfcc.shape
    if n_windows <= 1 or total <= T:
        return [pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)]
    max_start = max(0, total - T)
    starts = np.linspace(0, max_start, num=n_windows).astype(int)
    out = []
    for s in starts:
        out.append(mfcc[:, s:s+T] if s + T <= total else pad_or_crop(mfcc[:, s:], T))
    return out

def select_loudest_windows(windows: list, k: int):
    """Pick K windows with largest RMS over MFCC matrix (proxy for energy)."""
    if not windows or k is None or k <= 0 or k >= len(windows):
        return windows
    scores = [float(np.sqrt((w ** 2).mean())) for w in windows]  # <-- fixed
    idx = np.argsort(scores)[-k:]
    idx.sort()
    return [windows[i] for i in idx]

# ---------------- Inference helpers ----------------
def infer_single_batch(model: nn.Module, device, windows: list):
    if len(windows) == 0:
        raise ValueError("No windows to infer.")
    x = np.stack(windows, axis=0)        # (N,13,T)
    x = x[:, None, :, :]                 # (N,1,13,T)
    xt = torch.from_numpy(x).to(device)
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits = model(xt)               # (N,C)
        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1) # (N,C)
    return logits, probs

def aggregate(logits: torch.Tensor, probs: torch.Tensor, strategy: str):
    strategy = (strategy or "mean").lower()
    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        avg_logits = logits.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)   # (1,C)
        agg_probs = F.softmax(avg_logits, dim=1)        # (1,C)
        return agg_probs.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()
    elif strategy == "max":
        agg_probs = probs.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values
        s = agg_probs.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)
        agg_probs = agg_probs / s
        return agg_probs.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()
    else:
        agg_probs = probs.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)
        return agg_probs.squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()

# ---------------- Processed .npy discovery ----------------
def find_processed_npy_for_wav(processed_root: str, wav_path: str):
    if not processed_root or not os.path.isdir(processed_root):
        return None
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0].lower()
    for root, _, files in os.walk(processed_root):
        for fn in files:
            if fn.lower().endswith(".npy") and fn.lower().startswith(stem):
                return os.path.join(root, fn)
    return None

def load_processed_npy(npy_path: str):
    a = np.load(npy_path)
    if a.ndim == 3:
        a = a.squeeze(-1)
    if a.ndim != 2 or a.shape[0] != 13:
        raise ValueError(f"Processed MFCC must be (13,T[,*]); got shape {a.shape}")
    return a.astype(np.float32)

# ---------------- Main ----------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Infer frog species from a WAV file.")
    ap.add_argument("--wav", required=True, help="Path to audio .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--npy", help="(Optional) Path to processed MFCC .npy (13,T or 13,T,1)")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-only", action="store_true", help="Require a processed .npy; error if none found")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "models", "frog_cnn_best.pt"))
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "models", "label_map.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--topk", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=None, help="If top prob < threshold, print 'UNCERTAIN'")
    ap.add_argument("--normalize", action="store_true", help="(fallback librosa path) per-coef z-norm per clip")
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=1, help="# windows to evaluate")
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean", "max", "logit_mean", "logit-mean"], default="mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none", "loudest"], default="none", help="Keep only loudest windows (RMS)")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=None, help="# windows to keep if --focus loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "data", "processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=None, help="Path to mfcc_stats.json for dataset-level normalization")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    print("=== Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"WAV:      {args.wav}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Labels:   {args.label_map}")
    print(f"Frames:   {args.frames} | TopK: {args.topk}")

    # Load checkpoint
    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    ckpt_frames = None
    state_dict = None
    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if isinstance(ckpt.get("classes"), list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]
            print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from checkpoint.")
        if isinstance(ckpt.get("frames"), int):
            ckpt_frames = ckpt["frames"]
            print(f"Using target frames from checkpoint: {ckpt_frames}")
        state_dict = ckpt.get("model_state") or ckpt.get("state_dict")
        if state_dict is None:
            tensorish = {k: v for k, v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensorish if tensorish else ckpt
    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args.label_map)
        print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from label_map.json")
    n_classes = len(classes)

    target_frames = ckpt_frames if ckpt_frames is not None else args.frames

    # Build model and load weights
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=n_classes)
    if state_dict is None:
        state_dict = ckpt
    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    warn_msgs = []
    if missing:
        warn_msgs.append(f"missing: {list(missing)}")
    if unexpected:
        warn_msgs.append(f"unexpected: {list(unexpected)}")
    if warn_msgs:
        print("⚠️ load_state_dict warnings — " + ", ".join(warn_msgs))
    model.to(device)
    model.eval()

    # Feature source
    feature_source = None
    if args.npy:
        mfcc = load_processed_npy(args.npy)
        feature_source = "Processed (.npy, explicit)"
    else:
        auto_npy = find_processed_npy_for_wav(args.processed_root, args.wav)
        if auto_npy and os.path.isfile(auto_npy):
            mfcc = load_processed_npy(auto_npy)
            feature_source = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
        elif args.processed_only:
            raise FileNotFoundError("No processed .npy found (and --processed-only set).")
        else:
            mfcc = librosa_mfcc_13(
                args.wav, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None,
                center=True, htk=False, norm="ortho"
            )
            feature_source = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"

    # Normalize
    if args.mfcc_stats:
        try:
            mfcc = apply_dataset_norm(mfcc, args.mfcc_stats)
        except Exception as e:
            warnings.warn(f"mfcc_stats normalization failed: {e}")
    elif args.normalize:
        mfcc = apply_clip_znorm(mfcc)

    print(f"Feature source: {feature_source}")

    # Windows
    windows = segment_even_windows(mfcc, target_frames, max(1, int(args.multi)))
    if args.focus == "loudest" and args.focus_k:
        windows = select_loudest_windows(windows, args.focus_k)

    # Inference
    logits, probs = infer_single_batch(model, device, windows)
    agg = aggregate(logits, probs, strategy=args.strategy)  # (C,)

    # Top-K
    topk = max(1, min(args.topk, n_classes))
    idxs = np.argsort(-agg)[:topk]

    # Uncertainty message
    if args.uncertain_threshold is not None:
        top_prob = float(agg[idxs[0]])
        if top_prob < args.uncertain_threshold:
            print(f"\n🤔 UNCERTAIN — top confidence {top_prob*100:.2f}% (< {args.uncertain_threshold*100:.0f}%)\n")

    print("\nTop predictions:")
    for i in idxs:
        print(f"  {classes[i]:<30} {agg[i]*100:8.2f}%")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import os, json, argparse, glob, numpy as np
import librosa
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

# ---------------- Model ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),
        )
        # keep linear at indices 2 and 5 to match your checkpoint
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),            # 0
            nn.Identity(),           # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256), # 2
            nn.ReLU(),               # 3
            nn.Identity(),           # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes) # 5
        )
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Utils ----------------
def pad_or_crop(a, T):
    c, t = a.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
        a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
    elif t > T:
        a = a[:, :T]
    return a

def try_find_processed_npy(wav_path, processed_root):
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0]
    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(processed_root, "**", f"{stem}*.npy"), recursive=True)
    return hits[0] if hits else None

def load_mfcc_stats(stats_path):
    if stats_path and os.path.exists(stats_path):
        with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            js = json.load(f)
        mean = np.asarray(js.get("mean"), dtype=np.float32)
        std  = np.asarray(js.get("std"),  dtype=np.float32)
        if mean.shape == (13,) and std.shape == (13,) and np.all(std > 0):
            return mean, std
    return None, None

def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None, center=True, htk=False, norm='ortho'):
    y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(
        y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13,
        n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, win_length=win_length,
        center=center, htk=htk, norm=norm
    )
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)

def load_label_map_any(path, fallback=None):
    cls = None
    if path and os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lm = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
            cls = lm
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm and isinstance(lm["classes"], list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = lm["classes"]
            elif all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = [x["name"] for x in lm["classes"]]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k,str) for k in lm.keys()) and all(isinstance(v,int) for v in lm.values()):
            size = max(lm.values())+1
            cls = [None]*size
            for name, idx in lm.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size: cls[idx] = name
            if any(x is None for x in cls):
                cls = [k for k,_ in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v,str) for v in lm.values()):
            try:
                kv = [(int(k),v) for k,v in lm.items()]
                kv.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]); cls = [v for _,v in kv]
            except Exception:
                pass
    return cls if cls is not None else (fallback or [])

def window_rms(mfcc_win):
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mfcc_win**2)))

# ---------------- Inference over multiple windows ----------------
def infer_multi_windows(mfcc, model, frames, device, multi, strategy, focus, focus_k):
    T = frames
    total = mfcc.shape[1]
    if total <= T or multi <= 1:
        win = pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)[None, None, ...]
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = model(torch.from_numpy(win).to(device))
            probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()[0]
        return probs

    starts = np.linspace(0, max(total - T, 0), num=multi, dtype=int)
    windows = []
    for s in starts:
        sub = mfcc[:, s:s+T]
        sub = pad_or_crop(sub, T)
        windows.append(sub)

    if focus == "loudest":
        scores = [window_rms(w) for w in windows]
        keep_idx = np.argsort(scores)[-min(focus_k, len(windows)):]
        windows = [windows[i] for i in keep_idx]

    agg_logits = None
    agg_probs  = None
    for sub in windows:
        x = sub[None, None, ...]
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = model(torch.from_numpy(x).to(device))
            probs  = F.softmax(logits, dim=1)

        if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
            agg_logits = probs.new_zeros_like(logits) if agg_logits is None else agg_logits
            agg_logits += logits
        elif strategy == "max":
            agg_probs = probs if agg_probs is None else torch.maximum(agg_probs, probs)
        else:  # mean
            agg_probs = probs if agg_probs is None else agg_probs + probs

    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        probs = F.softmax(agg_logits / len(windows), dim=1).cpu().numpy()[0]
    elif strategy == "max":
        probs = agg_probs.cpu().numpy()[0]
    else:
        probs = (agg_probs / len(windows)).cpu().numpy()[0]
    return probs

# ---------------- Main ----------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Infer frog species from a WAV file.")
    ap.add_argument("--wav", required=True, help="Path to audio .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--npy", help="(Optional) Path to processed MFCC .npy (13,T or 13,T,1)")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-only", action="store_true", help="Require a processed .npy; error if none found")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","frog_cnn_best.pt"))
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","label_map.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--topk", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--normalize", action="store_true", help="(fallback librosa path) per-coef z-norm per clip")
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=12, help="# windows to evaluate")
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean","max","logit_mean","logit-mean"], default="mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none","loudest"], default="none", help="Keep only loudest windows (RMS)")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=6, help="# windows to keep if --focus loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","data","processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","mfcc_stats.json"))
    args = ap.parse_args()

    print("=== Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"WAV:      {args.wav}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Labels:   {args.label_map}")
    print(f"Frames:   {args.frames} | TopK: {args.topk}")

    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    target_frames = args.frames
    state_dict = None

    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if "classes" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["classes"], list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]; print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from checkpoint.")
        if "frames" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["frames"], int):
            target_frames = ckpt["frames"]; print(f"Using target frames from checkpoint: {target_frames}")
        if "model_state" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["model_state"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["model_state"]
        elif "state_dict" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["state_dict"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["state_dict"]

    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args.label_map)
        print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from label_map.json")
    n_classes = len(classes)

    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=n_classes)
    if state_dict is None:
        if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
            tensor_only = {k:v for k,v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensor_only if tensor_only else ckpt
        else:
            state_dict = ckpt
    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    if missing or unexpected:
        print(f"⚠️ load_state_dict warnings — missing: {list(missing)}, unexpected: {list(unexpected)}")

    device = torch.device(args.device); model.to(device); model.eval()

    # -------- Feature source --------
    if args.npy:
        if not os.path.exists(args.npy):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"--npy not found: {args.npy}")
        a = np.load(args.npy).astype(np.float32)
        if a.ndim == 3: a = a.squeeze(-1)
        if a.shape[0] != 13:
            raise ValueError(f"--npy has unexpected shape {a.shape}, expected (13,T) or (13,T,1)")
        mfcc = a
        feature_src = "Processed (.npy, explicit)"
    else:
        npy_match = try_find_processed_npy(args.wav, args.processed_root)
        if npy_match and os.path.exists(npy_match):
            a = np.load(npy_match).astype(np.float32)
            if a.ndim == 3: a = a.squeeze(-1)
            if a.shape[0] != 13:
                raise ValueError(f"Processed file has unexpected shape: {a.shape}")
            mfcc = a
            feature_src = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
        else:
            if args.processed_only:
                raise FileNotFoundError("No matching processed .npy found and --processed-only set.")
            mfcc = librosa_mfcc_13(args.wav, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160)
            if args.normalize:
                mfcc = (mfcc - mfcc.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)) / (mfcc.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6)
            mean, std = load_mfcc_stats(args.mfcc_stats)
            if mean is not None and std is not None:
                mfcc = (mfcc - mean[:,None]) / (std[:,None] + 1e-6)
            feature_src = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"

    print(f"Feature source: {feature_src}")

    # -------- Inference --------
    probs = infer_multi_windows(
        mfcc, model, target_frames, device,
        multi=max(1, args.multi), strategy=args.strategy,
        focus=args.focus, focus_k=args.focus_k
    )

    topk = min(args.topk, n_classes)
    idxs = np.argsort(-probs)[:topk]
    print("\nTop predictions:")
    for i in idxs:
        print(f"  {classes[i]:<32} {probs[i]*100:6.2f}%")

    if args.uncertain_threshold is not None:
        top = probs[idxs[0]] * 100.0
        if top < args.uncertain_threshold:
            print(f"\n🤔 UNCERTAIN — top confidence {top:.2f}% (< {args.uncertain_threshold:.0f}%)")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
ChatGPT said:
Thought for 23s

You’ve still got the old bug in infer_multi_windows: it tries to init with probs.new_zeros_like(logits) (which doesn’t exist). Below is a clean, fixed, and slightly tightened version of your script. The only behavioral change is the logits-mean aggregation is now correct and stable.

Just replace your file with this:

import os, json, argparse, glob as _glob, numpy as np
import librosa
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

# ---------------- Model ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64*13*1 = 832 features
        )
        # keep linear at indices 2 and 5 to match your checkpoint
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),             # 0
            nn.Identity(),            # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256),  # 2
            nn.ReLU(),                # 3
            nn.Identity(),            # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes) # 5
        )
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Utils ----------------
def pad_or_crop(a, T):
    c, t = a.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
        a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
    elif t > T:
        a = a[:, :T]
    return a

def try_find_processed_npy(wav_path, processed_root):
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0]
    hits = _glob.glob(os.path.join(processed_root, "**", f"{stem}*.npy"), recursive=True)
    return hits[0] if hits else None

def load_mfcc_stats(stats_path):
    if stats_path and os.path.exists(stats_path):
        with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            js = json.load(f)
        mean = np.asarray(js.get("mean"), dtype=np.float32) if js.get("mean") is not None else None
        std  = np.asarray(js.get("std"),  dtype=np.float32) if js.get("std")  is not None else None
        if mean is not None and std is not None and mean.shape == (13,) and std.shape == (13,) and np.all(std > 0):
            return mean, std
    return None, None

def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None, center=True, htk=False, norm='ortho'):
    y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(
        y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13,
        n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, win_length=win_length,
        center=center, htk=htk, norm=norm
    )
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)

def load_label_map_any(path, fallback=None):
    cls = None
    if path and os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lm = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
            cls = lm
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm and isinstance(lm["classes"], list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = lm["classes"]
            elif all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = [x["name"] for x in lm["classes"]]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k,str) for k in lm.keys()) and all(isinstance(v,int) for v in lm.values()):
            size = max(lm.values())+1
            cls = [None]*size
            for name, idx in lm.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size: cls[idx] = name
            if any(x is None for x in cls):
                cls = [k for k,_ in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v,str) for v in lm.values()):
            try:
                kv = [(int(k),v) for k,v in lm.items()]
                kv.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]); cls = [v for _,v in kv]
            except Exception:
                pass
    return cls if cls is not None else (fallback or [])

def window_rms(mfcc_win):
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mfcc_win**2)))

# ---------------- Inference over multiple windows ----------------
def infer_multi_windows(mfcc, model, frames, device, multi, strategy, focus, focus_k):
    T = frames
    total = mfcc.shape[1]
    if total <= T or multi <= 1:
        win = pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)[None, None, ...]  # (1,1,13,T)
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = model(torch.from_numpy(win).to(device))
            probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()[0]
        return probs

    starts = np.linspace(0, max(total - T, 0), num=multi, dtype=int)
    windows = []
    for s in starts:
        sub = mfcc[:, s:s+T]
        sub = pad_or_crop(sub, T)
        windows.append(sub)

    if focus == "loudest" and focus_k is not None and focus_k > 0:
        k = min(focus_k, len(windows))
        scores = [window_rms(w) for w in windows]
        keep_idx = np.argsort(scores)[-k:]
        keep_idx.sort()
        windows = [windows[i] for i in keep_idx]

    agg_logits = None
    agg_probs  = None
    for sub in windows:
        x = sub[None, None, ...]  # (1,1,13,T)
        xt = torch.from_numpy(x).to(device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = model(xt)           # (1,C)
            probs  = F.softmax(logits, dim=1)  # (1,C)

        if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
            # FIX: initialize with zeros_like(logits), not probs.new_zeros_like(logits)
            agg_logits = torch.zeros_like(logits) if agg_logits is None else agg_logits
            agg_logits += logits
        elif strategy == "max":
            agg_probs = probs if agg_probs is None else torch.maximum(agg_probs, probs)
        else:  # mean
            agg_probs = probs if agg_probs is None else agg_probs + probs

    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        avg_logits = agg_logits / len(windows)
        probs = F.softmax(avg_logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()[0]
    elif strategy == "max":
        probs = agg_probs.cpu().numpy()[0]
    else:
        probs = (agg_probs / len(windows)).cpu().numpy()[0]
    return probs

# ---------------- Main ----------------

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Short answer: yes—the pipeline is fine and, based on your earlier eval (~0.80 top-1 val accuracy, macro-F1 ~0.75), it will do well on many species. What you’re seeing with that acris_blanchardi clip is a per-class weakness, not a broken infer script.

Why this one struggles

In your per-class report, acris_blanchardi had low recall/F1. So the model often confuses it with similar “peeping/chirping” species.

Your predictions include species from other continents (e.g., Nidirana, Litoria)—a classic case where no geographic prior causes confident but irrelevant guesses.

Quick wins for real-world use

Geographic whitelist at inference (or in the app): zero out probs for species that can’t occur at the recording location, then renormalize. This alone will clean up many wrong “confident” picks.

Keep your uncertainty threshold on (good for field use).

When scanning long audio, use --multi with --strategy max and optionally --focus loudest to grab the best window (you tried this—good).

If you want it to learn acris_blanchardi better

Add more examples for that class (or targeted oversampling).

Use mixup/SpecAugment and light background-noise mixing.

Try focal loss (helps tail classes).

Consider a slightly stronger head (e.g., a small CRNN) and/or longer frames (150–200) with overlapping windows at train time.

Awesome — here’s a tiny, safe patch to add a region whitelist to your existing infer_wav.py. It lets you pass a text/JSON file of allowed species; everything else gets zeroed out and the remaining probs are renormalized.

1) Add this helper (near your other utils)
# ----- Region whitelist loader -----
def load_whitelist(path):
    """
    Accepts:
      - TXT: one class name per line
      - JSON: either a list of class names, or an object with key 'allow'/'whitelist'
    Returns a set of allowed class names (exact match to your label_map/classes).
    """
    if not path or not os.path.exists(path):
        return None
    try:
        if path.lower().endswith(".json"):
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
            if isinstance(data, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in data):
                return set(data)
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                for k in ("allow", "whitelist"):
                    if k in data and isinstance(data[k], list):
                        return set(str(x) for x in data[k])
        # default: treat as newline text
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            names = [ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()]
        return set(names) if names else None
    except Exception:
        return None

2) Add a CLI flag (in your argparse block)
ap.add_argument("--whitelist", default=None,
                help="Path to TXT/JSON list of allowed species; others are masked out & renormalized")

3) Apply the mask after you compute probs (right before you pick Top-K)
# -------- Inference --------
probs = infer_multi_windows(
    mfcc, model, target_frames, device,
    multi=max(1, args.multi), strategy=args.strategy,
    focus=args.focus, focus_k=args.focus_k
)

# --- Region whitelist (optional) ---
allowed = load_whitelist(args.whitelist)
if allowed:
    keep = np.array([name in allowed for name in classes], dtype=np.float32)
    masked = probs * keep
    kept = int(keep.sum())
    if masked.sum() > 0 and kept > 0:
        probs = masked / masked.sum()
        print(f"Applied whitelist: kept {kept}/{len(classes)} classes.")
    else:
        print("Whitelist provided, but none of its names matched your model classes — leaving probabilities unfiltered.")

topk = min(args.topk, n_classes)
idxs = np.argsort(-probs)[:topk]
print("\nTop predictions:")
for i in idxs:
    print(f"  {classes[i]:<32} {probs[i]*100:6.2f}%")

How to use it

Create a file with local species names (exactly as in your model’s classes / label_map.json). Example FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist.txt:

acris_blanchardi
pseudacris_maculata
hyla_versicolor
anaxyrus_americanus
lithobates_clamitans
lithobates_catesbeianus
gastrophryne_olivacea
...


Run inference with the whitelist:

python FrogSoundProject\src\infer_wav.py `
  --wav "C:\path\to\audio.wav" `
  --model "FrogSoundProject\models\frog_cnn_best.pt" `
  --label-map "FrogSoundProject\models\label_map.json" `
  --frames 100 `
  --topk 5 `
  --multi 12 `
  --strategy mean `
  --whitelist "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist.txt"


If you want a JSON version, this also works:

{
  "whitelist": [
    "acris_blanchardi",
    "pseudacris_maculata",
    "hyla_versicolor"
  ]
}

Notes

Names must match exactly whatever your model uses (print classes once to confirm).

If nothing matches, the script prints a warning and leaves probs unchanged.

This doesn’t require retraining; it just post-processes outputs to respect geography, which should immediately improve practical accuracy for your region.

Got it—here’s a single, clean infer_wav.py that includes the region whitelist (TXT/JSON), all the multi-window options, and the earlier bug fixes. Under the script you’ll find short Windows/PowerShell instructions.

FrogSoundProject/src/infer_wav.py
import os, json, argparse, glob, numpy as np
import librosa
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

# ---------------- Model ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            # Pool to (13,1) so 64*13*1 = 832 features -> matches checkpoint head shape we trained with
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),
        )
        # Keep Linear layers at indices 2 and 5 to match your checkpoint naming
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),            # 0
            nn.Identity(),           # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256), # 2
            nn.ReLU(),               # 3
            nn.Identity(),           # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes) # 5
        )
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Utils ----------------
def pad_or_crop(a, T):
    c, t = a.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
        a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
    elif t > T:
        a = a[:, :T]
    return a

def try_find_processed_npy(wav_path, processed_root):
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0]
    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(processed_root, "**", f"{stem}*.npy"), recursive=True)
    return hits[0] if hits else None

def load_mfcc_stats(stats_path):
    if stats_path and os.path.exists(stats_path):
        with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            js = json.load(f)
        mean = np.asarray(js.get("mean"), dtype=np.float32)
        std  = np.asarray(js.get("std"),  dtype=np.float32)
        if mean.shape == (13,) and std.shape == (13,) and np.all(std > 0):
            return mean, std
    return None, None

def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None, center=True, htk=False, norm='ortho'):
    # Load to mono at target SR
    y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(
        y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13,
        n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, win_length=win_length,
        center=center, htk=htk, norm=norm
    )
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)

def load_label_map_any(path, fallback=None):
    """Accepts: list, {'classes':[...]}, name->idx, idx->name, or simple txt (one per line)"""
    cls = None
    if path and os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lm = json.load(f)
        # list
        if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
            cls = lm
        # {"classes":[...]} or {"classes":[{"name":...}, ...]}
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm and isinstance(lm["classes"], list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = lm["classes"]
            elif all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = [x["name"] for x in lm["classes"]]
        # name -> idx
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k,str) for k in lm.keys()) and all(isinstance(v,int) for v in lm.values()):
            size = max(lm.values())+1
            cls = [None]*size
            for name, idx in lm.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size: cls[idx] = name
            if any(x is None for x in cls):
                cls = [k for k,_ in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
        # idx (str/int) -> name
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v,str) for v in lm.values()):
            try:
                kv = [(int(k),v) for k,v in lm.items()]
                kv.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]); cls = [v for _,v in kv]
            except Exception:
                pass
    return cls if cls is not None else (fallback or [])

def window_rms(mfcc_win):
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mfcc_win**2)))

# ----- Region whitelist loader -----
def load_whitelist(path):
    """
    Accepts:
      - TXT: one class name per line
      - JSON: either a list of class names, or an object with key 'allow'/'whitelist'
    Returns a set of allowed class names (exact match to your label_map/classes), or None.
    """
    if not path or not os.path.exists(path):
        return None
    try:
        if path.lower().endswith(".json"):
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
            if isinstance(data, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in data):
                return set(data)
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                for k in ("allow", "whitelist"):
                    if k in data and isinstance(data[k], list):
                        return set(str(x) for x in data[k])
        # default: newline text
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            names = [ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()]
        return set(names) if names else None
    except Exception:
        return None

# ---------------- Inference over multiple windows ----------------
def infer_multi_windows(mfcc, model, frames, device, multi, strategy, focus, focus_k):
    T = frames
    total = mfcc.shape[1]
    # Single window fast path
    if total <= T or multi <= 1:
        win = pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)[None, None, ...]  # (1,1,13,T)
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = model(torch.from_numpy(win).to(device))
            probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()[0]
        return probs

    # Build evenly spaced windows
    starts = np.linspace(0, max(total - T, 0), num=multi, dtype=int)
    windows = []
    for s in starts:
        sub = mfcc[:, s:s+T]
        sub = pad_or_crop(sub, T)
        windows.append(sub)

    # Focus: keep loudest K windows by RMS
    if focus == "loudest":
        scores = [window_rms(w) for w in windows]
        keep_idx = np.argsort(scores)[-min(focus_k, len(windows)):]
        windows = [windows[i] for i in keep_idx]

    agg_logits = None
    agg_probs  = None
    used = 0
    for sub in windows:
        x = sub[None, None, ...]
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = model(torch.from_numpy(x).to(device))
            probs  = F.softmax(logits, dim=1)

        if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
            agg_logits = torch.zeros_like(logits) if agg_logits is None else agg_logits
            agg_logits += logits
        elif strategy == "max":
            agg_probs = probs if agg_probs is None else torch.maximum(agg_probs, probs)
        else:  # mean of probs
            agg_probs = probs if agg_probs is None else (agg_probs + probs)
        used += 1

    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        probs = F.softmax(agg_logits / max(1, used), dim=1).cpu().numpy()[0]
    elif strategy == "max":
        probs = agg_probs.cpu().numpy()[0]
    else:
        probs = (agg_probs / max(1, used)).cpu().numpy()[0]
    return probs

# ---------------- Main ----------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Infer frog species from a WAV file.")
    ap.add_argument("--wav", required=True, help="Path to audio .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--npy", help="(Optional) Path to processed MFCC .npy (13,T or 13,T,1)")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-only", action="store_true", help="Require a processed .npy; error if none found")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","frog_cnn_best.pt"))
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","label_map.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--topk", type=int, default=5)
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=None, help="If top prob < threshold, print 'UNCERTAIN'")
    ap.add_argument("--normalize", action="store_true", help="(librosa path) per-coef z-norm per clip")
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=12, help="# windows to evaluate")
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean","max","logit_mean","logit-mean"], default="mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none","loudest"], default="none", help="Keep only loudest windows (RMS)")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=6, help="# windows to keep if --focus loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--whitelist", default=None, help="TXT/JSON of allowed species; others masked & renormalized")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","data","processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","mfcc_stats.json"))
    args = ap.parse_args()

    print("=== Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"WAV:      {args.wav}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Labels:   {args.label_map}")
    print(f"Frames:   {args.frames} | TopK: {args.topk}")

    # -------- Load checkpoint / metadata --------
    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    target_frames = args.frames
    state_dict = None

    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if "classes" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["classes"], list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]; print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from checkpoint.")
        if "frames" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["frames"], int):
            target_frames = ckpt["frames"]; print(f"Using target frames from checkpoint: {target_frames}")
        if "model_state" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["model_state"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["model_state"]
        elif "state_dict" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["state_dict"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["state_dict"]

    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args.label_map)
        print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from label_map.json")
    n_classes = len(classes)

    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=n_classes)
    if state_dict is None:
        if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
            tensor_only = {k:v for k,v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensor_only if tensor_only else ckpt
        else:
            state_dict = ckpt
    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    if missing or unexpected:
        print(f"⚠️ load_state_dict warnings — missing: {list(missing)}, unexpected: {list(unexpected)}")

    device = torch.device(args.device)
    model.to(device); model.eval()

    # -------- Feature source --------
    if args.npy:
        if not os.path.exists(args.npy):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"--npy not found: {args.npy}")
        a = np.load(args.npy).astype(np.float32)
        if a.ndim == 3: a = a.squeeze(-1)
        if a.shape[0] != 13:
            raise ValueError(f"--npy has unexpected shape {a.shape}, expected (13,T) or (13,T,1)")
        mfcc = a
        feature_src = "Processed (.npy, explicit)"
    else:
        npy_match = try_find_processed_npy(args.wav, args.processed_root)
        if npy_match and os.path.exists(npy_match):
            a = np.load(npy_match).astype(np.float32)
            if a.ndim == 3: a = a.squeeze(-1)
            if a.shape[0] != 13:
                raise ValueError(f"Processed file has unexpected shape: {a.shape}")
            mfcc = a
            feature_src = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
        else:
            if args.processed_only:
                raise FileNotFoundError("No matching processed .npy found and --processed-only set.")
            mfcc = librosa_mfcc_13(args.wav, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160)
            if args.normalize:
                mfcc = (mfcc - mfcc.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)) / (mfcc.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6)
            mean, std = load_mfcc_stats(args.mfcc_stats)
            if mean is not None and std is not None:
                mfcc = (mfcc - mean[:,None]) / (std[:,None] + 1e-6)
            feature_src = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"

    print(f"Feature source: {feature_src}")

    # -------- Inference --------
    probs = infer_multi_windows(
        mfcc, model, target_frames, device,
        multi=max(1, args.multi), strategy=args.strategy,
        focus=args.focus, focus_k=args.focus_k
    )

    # --- Region whitelist (optional) ---
    allowed = load_whitelist(args.whitelist)
    if allowed:
        keep_mask = np.array([name in allowed for name in classes], dtype=np.float32)
        masked = probs * keep_mask
        kept = int(keep_mask.sum())
        if masked.sum() > 0 and kept > 0:
            probs = masked / masked.sum()
            print(f"Applied whitelist: kept {kept}/{len(classes)} classes.")
        else:
            print("Whitelist provided, but none matched your model classes — leaving probabilities unfiltered.")

    # Print Top-K
    topk = min(args.topk, n_classes)
    idxs = np.argsort(-probs)[:topk]
    print("\nTop predictions:")
    for i in idxs:
        print(f"  {classes[i]:<32} {probs[i]*100:6.2f}%")

    # Uncertainty gate (optional)
    if args.uncertain_threshold is not None:
        top = probs[idxs[0]] * 100.0
        if top < args.uncertain_threshold:
            print(f"\n🤔 UNCERTAIN — top confidence {top:.2f}% (< {args.uncertain_threshold:.0f}%)")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

How to use (Windows / PowerShell)

Create a whitelist of local species (names must match your model’s class names exactly).
Example FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist.txt:

acris_blanchardi
pseudacris_maculata
hyla_versicolor
anaxyrus_americanus
lithobates_clamitans
lithobates_catesbeianus
gastrophryne_olivacea


(JSON also works; either a list or {"whitelist": [ ... ]}.)

Run inference (multi-window + whitelist):

$wav = "C:\Users\dhruv\OneDrive\Desktop\Frog Sound Project\FrogSoundProject\data\raw\inat_frogs\acris_blanchardi\acris_blanchardi_obs302690303_s1588472.wav"

python FrogSoundProject\src\infer_wav.py `
  --wav "$wav" `
  --model "FrogSoundProject\models\frog_cnn_best.pt" `
  --label-map "FrogSoundProject\models\label_map.json" `
  --frames 100 `
  --topk 5 `
  --multi 12 `
  --strategy mean `
  --whitelist "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist.txt" `
  --uncertain-threshold 70


For loudest-window focus (optional):

--focus loudest --focus-k 6


To rely on your precomputed MFCC stats (if you made them):

--mfcc-stats "FrogSoundProject\models\mfcc_stats.json"


If your .npy (processed MFCC) exists and you want to force using it:

$npy = "C:\Users\dhruv\OneDrive\Desktop\Frog Sound Project\FrogSoundProject\data\processed\acris_blanchardi\acris_blanchardi_obs302690303_s1588472.npy"
python FrogSoundProject\src\infer_wav.py --wav "$wav" --npy "$npy" --model ... --label-map ... --frames 100 --topk 5 --multi 12 --strategy mean --whitelist "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist.txt"

Tips

If results still feel off for a known species, double-check class names in your label_map.json or checkpoint and ensure the whitelist matches exactly (case, underscores).

The whitelist only masks outputs; it doesn’t change the model. It immediately improves practical accuracy by removing out-of-region species.

For short chirps, try --multi 20 --focus loudest --focus-k 8 to emphasize louder segments. For longer calls, --multi 40 can help.

If you see very low confidences across all classes, it usually means the audio window doesn’t capture a canonical call segment (or the call is rare/underrepresented). Multi-window + loudest focus helps.

Your whitelist didn’t actually filter anything—that “Applied whitelist: kept 65/65 classes” line means the file you passed contains all 65 class names (or at least it matched all of them). So the model still had the full label set, which is why you’re seeing those non-local species.

Here’s a quick fix + a sanity check.

1) Sanity-check what the model’s class names are

If the calls are intermittent, this combo often helps:

(You can keep the whitelist on at the same time.)

Why this matters

Without a whitelist the network happily assigns probability mass to non-local classes (e.g., Nidirana, Kaloula) it saw during training. A regional mask + renormalization is a practical way to boost accuracy on your own data without retraining.

Nice—thanks for dumping the class list and trying the whitelist 👌

Here’s what your last run tells us:

The whitelist worked (kept 10/65), so masking is fine.

With those 10, the model still goes ~99.9% for gastrophryne_olivacea on that clip. When you force a 1-species whitelist (acris_blanchardi only), you obviously get 100%—so the plumbing is OK. The issue is the model’s belief for that clip, not the code.

Why it’s picking the “wrong” species

This specific recording’s MFCC pattern is landing much closer to the learned template for Gastrophryne than for Acris. Common reasons:

Label noise / look-alike calls in training—Acris vs Gastrophryne can overlap in chirp rate/band emphasis depending on SNR and background.

Distribution mismatch between how features were normalized while training and during inference (per-clip vs global stats).

Short windowing (100 frames ≈ ~1 s at hop 10 ms) can miss the pattern over time that disambiguates these two.

You’ve already tried a lot of the right toggles. Here are concrete things that tend to help the Acris vs Gastrophryne confusion:

Freeze features (the conv stack), train only the two Linear layers for a few epochs on your processed set filtered to those 10 labels.

loads frog_cnn_best.pt

remaps labels to a whitelist subset

freezes convs, trains the classifier head

saves frog_cnn_head_ft.pt + a new label map (the 10 classes)

2) Full script: FrogSoundProject/src/finetune_head.py
# FrogSoundProject/src/finetune_head.py
import os, json, argparse, random, numpy as np
from glob import glob

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader, WeightedRandomSampler

# ---------------- Model (same layout as your infer script) ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64*13*1 = 832
        )
        # classifier indices 2 and 5 match your checkpoint head
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),                 # 0
            nn.Identity(),                # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256),      # 2
            nn.ReLU(),                    # 3
            nn.Identity(),                # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes)     # 5
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Dataset ----------------
class MFCCDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, items, target_frames=100):
        self.items = items
        self.T = target_frames

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.items)

    def _pad_or_crop(self, a):
        c, t = a.shape
        if t < self.T:
            pad = np.zeros((c, self.T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
            a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
        elif t > self.T:
            a = a[:, :self.T]
        return a

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        path, y = self.items[idx]
        a = np.load(path)
        if a.ndim == 3:  # (13, T, 1)
            a = a.squeeze(-1)
        a = a.astype(np.float32)
        a = self._pad_or_crop(a)         # (13, T)
        a = a[None, ...]                 # (1, 13, T)
        return torch.from_numpy(a), y

# ---------------- Helpers ----------------
def read_whitelist(path):
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return [ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()]

def build_all_items(proc_dir, classes):
    items = []
    for i, cls in enumerate(classes):
        for fp in glob(os.path.join(proc_dir, cls, "*.npy")):
            items.append((fp, i))
    return items

def split_items(items, train_split=0.78, seed=1337):
    rnd = random.Random(seed)
    rnd.shuffle(items)
    n_train = int(len(items) * train_split)
    return items[:n_train], items[n_train:]

def class_counts(items, n_classes):
    cnt = [0]*n_classes
    for _, y in items:
        cnt[y] += 1
    return cnt

# ---------------- Train / Eval ----------------
def train_one_epoch(model, loader, device, optimizer, criterion):
    model.train()
    total_loss, total_correct, total = 0.0, 0, 0
    for xb, yb in loader:
        xb = xb.to(device)
        yb = yb.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(xb)
        loss = criterion(logits, yb)
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()

        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)
        pred = logits.argmax(dim=1)
        total_correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()
        total += xb.size(0)
    return total_loss/total, total_correct/total

@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model, loader, device, criterion):
    model.eval()
    total_loss, total_correct, total = 0.0, 0, 0
    for xb, yb in loader:
        xb = xb.to(device)
        yb = yb.to(device)
        logits = model(xb)
        loss = criterion(logits, yb)
        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)
        pred = logits.argmax(dim=1)
        total_correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()
        total += xb.size(0)
    return total_loss/total, total_correct/total

# ---------------- Main ----------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Fast head fine-tune on a species whitelist.")
    ap.add_argument("--processed", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","data","processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--checkpoint", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","frog_cnn_best.pt"))
    ap.add_argument("--whitelist", required=True, help="Path to newline list of species to keep")
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=8)
    ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=64)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=5e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--weight-decay", type=float, default=1e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--train-split", type=float, default=0.78)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1337)
    ap.add_argument("--balanced", action="store_true", help="Use class-balanced sampler")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","frog_cnn_head_ft.pt"))
    args = ap.parse_args()

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")

    # Load base checkpoint (get full class list + frames)
    ckpt = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location="cpu")
    base_classes = ckpt["classes"]
    base_frames = ckpt.get("frames", args.frames)
    state_dict = ckpt.get("model_state", ckpt.get("state_dict", ckpt))
    print(f"Base checkpoint: {len(base_classes)} classes | frames={base_frames}")

    # Read whitelist and intersect with base classes (preserve base order)
    want = set(read_whitelist(args.whitelist))
    classes = [c for c in base_classes if c in want]
    if len(classes) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Whitelist must contain at least 2 classes present in the checkpoint.")
    print(f"Whitelist kept {len(classes)}/{len(base_classes)} classes.")

    # Build dataset items using ONLY whitelisted classes
    items = []
    name_to_idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(classes)}
    for name in classes:
        folder = os.path.join(args.processed, name)
        for fp in glob(os.path.join(folder, "*.npy")):
            items.append((fp, name_to_idx[name]))

    if len(items) == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("No processed .npy files found for whitelist classes.")

    train_items, val_items = split_items(items, train_split=args.train_split, seed=args.seed)
    print(f"Train items: {len(train_items)} | Val items: {len(val_items)}")

    train_ds = MFCCDataset(train_items, target_frames=base_frames)
    val_ds   = MFCCDataset(val_items,   target_frames=base_frames)

    if args.balanced:
        cnt = class_counts(train_items, len(classes))
        weights = []
        for _, y in train_items:
            w = 1.0 / max(cnt[y], 1)
            weights.append(w)
        sampler = WeightedRandomSampler(weights, num_samples=len(train_items), replacement=True)
        train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=args.batch, sampler=sampler, num_workers=0, pin_memory=True)
    else:
        train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=args.batch, shuffle=True, num_workers=0, pin_memory=True)

    val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=args.batch, shuffle=False, num_workers=0, pin_memory=True)

    # Build model for SUBSET classes, load conv weights, freeze features
    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=len(classes))
    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    if missing or unexpected:
        print(f"load_state_dict: missing={list(missing)} | unexpected={list(unexpected)}")

    for p in model.features.parameters():
        p.requires_grad = False  # freeze conv stack

    # Train only classifier params
    params = [p for p in model.classifier.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=args.weight_decay)
    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

    model.to(device)

    best_acc, best_state = 0.0, None
    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs+1):
        tr_loss, tr_acc = train_one_epoch(model, train_loader, device, optimizer, criterion)
        va_loss, va_acc = evaluate(model, val_loader, device, criterion)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch:02d}/{args.epochs}  train_loss {tr_loss:.4f}  train_acc {tr_acc:.3f}  val_loss {va_loss:.4f}  val_acc {va_acc:.3f}")

        if va_acc >= best_acc:
            best_acc = va_acc
            best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}

    # Save best
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.out), exist_ok=True)
    out = {
        "model_state": best_state if best_state is not None else model.state_dict(),
        "classes": classes,
        "frames": int(base_frames),
        "note": "head-finetune on whitelist subset"
    }
    torch.save(out, args.out)
    lm = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(classes)}
    with open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(args.out), "label_map_subset.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(lm, f, indent=2)
    print(f"\n✅ Saved fine-tuned model -> {args.out}")
    print("✅ Saved label map ->", os.path.join(os.path.dirname(args.out), "label_map_subset.json"))
    print(f"Best val acc: {best_acc:.3f}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Why this works

The conv stack already learned “frog-ish” features. Your errors were class space + head calibration issues.

By narrowing the class space to plausible species and retraining only the head, we let the softmax devote probability mass to the right neighborhood instead of dozens of unlikely exotics.

It’s fast, reproducible, and immediately usable in the app.

If you later want to squeeze even more: bump frames to 200–300 and fine-tune again (same script, pass --frames 200 and regenerate your processed data accordingly). But try the head-tune first—you’ll typically see the biggest jump with the least hassle.
