Awesome use-case. Here’s a drop-in batch + multi-species “field mode” script that:

scans a folder of .wav files (recursively)

handles multi-species per recording via sliding windows + per-window thresholds

supports local whitelist (e.g., Oakville set) with a 2-pass fallback to all classes for invasive/unknown checks

writes a tidy CSV report with top-k, uncertainty flag, species present, and optional alert list hits

uses your existing model + MFCC pipeline (no re-training required)

batch_infer.py

Save to: FrogSoundProject/src/batch_infer.py

import os, sys, json, argparse, csv, glob, math
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional
import numpy as np
import librosa
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

# ---------------- Model (matches your checkpoint layout) ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64*13*1
        )
        # keep linear at indices 2 and 5 to match saved checkpoint
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),             # 0
            nn.Identity(),            # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256),  # 2
            nn.ReLU(),                # 3
            nn.Identity(),            # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes) # 5
        )
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Utils ----------------
def load_label_map_any(path: Optional[str], fallback=None) -> List[str]:
    cls = None
    if path and os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lm = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
            cls = lm
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm and isinstance(lm["classes"], list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = lm["classes"]
            elif all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = [x["name"] for x in lm["classes"]]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k, str) for k in lm.keys()) and all(isinstance(v, int) for v in lm.values()):
            size = max(lm.values()) + 1
            tmp = [None]*size
            for name, idx in lm.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size:
                    tmp[idx] = name
            if any(x is None for x in tmp):
                tmp = [k for k,_ in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
            cls = tmp
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in lm.values()):
            try:
                kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm.items()]
                kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                cls = [v for _, v in kv]
            except Exception:
                pass
    return cls if cls is not None else (fallback or [])

def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160, win_length=None, center=True, htk=False, norm='ortho'):
    y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(
        y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13,
        n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, win_length=win_length,
        center=center, htk=htk, norm=norm
    )
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32), float(len(y)/sr_target)

def pad_or_crop(a: np.ndarray, T: int) -> np.ndarray:
    c, t = a.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
        a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
    elif t > T:
        a = a[:, :T]
    return a

def try_find_processed_npy(wav_path: str, processed_root: str) -> Optional[str]:
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0]
    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(processed_root, "**", f"{stem}*.npy"), recursive=True)
    return hits[0] if hits else None

def load_mfcc_stats(stats_path: Optional[str]):
    if stats_path and os.path.exists(stats_path):
        with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            js = json.load(f)
        mean = np.asarray(js.get("mean"), dtype=np.float32)
        std  = np.asarray(js.get("std"),  dtype=np.float32)
        if mean.shape == (13,) and std.shape == (13,) and np.all(std > 0):
            return mean, std
    return None, None

def window_rms(mfcc_win: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mfcc_win**2)))

def build_windows(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int, multi: int) -> List[np.ndarray]:
    total = mfcc.shape[1]
    if total <= T or multi <= 1:
        return [pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)]
    starts = np.linspace(0, max(total - T, 0), num=multi, dtype=int)
    wins = []
    for s in starts:
        sub = mfcc[:, s:s+T]
        wins.append(pad_or_crop(sub, T))
    return wins

def apply_whitelist(probs: np.ndarray, full_classes: List[str], whitelist: Optional[set]) -> np.ndarray:
    if not whitelist:
        return probs
    mask = np.array([1.0 if name in whitelist else 0.0 for name in full_classes], dtype=np.float32)
    p = probs * mask
    s = p.sum()
    if s > 0:
        p = p / s
    return p

def topk_from_probs(probs: np.ndarray, classes: List[str], k: int) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
    idxs = np.argsort(-probs)[:k]
    return [(classes[i], float(probs[i])) for i in idxs]

# Batch a list of (N, 1, 13, T) windows for speed
def model_probs_for_windows(model: nn.Module, device, windows: List[np.ndarray], batch_size: int = 64) -> np.ndarray:
    if len(windows) == 0:
        return np.zeros((0, model.classifier[-1].out_features), dtype=np.float32)
    arr = np.stack([w[None, ...] for w in windows], axis=0)  # (W,1,13,T)
    W = arr.shape[0]
    probs_list = []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for i in range(0, W, batch_size):
            xb = torch.from_numpy(arr[i:i+batch_size]).to(device)
            logits = model(xb)
            pb = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            probs_list.append(pb)
    return np.concatenate(probs_list, axis=0)  # (W,C)

def aggregate_probs(per_window_probs: np.ndarray, strategy: str) -> np.ndarray:
    # per_window_probs: (W, C)
    if per_window_probs.shape[0] == 0:
        return np.zeros((per_window_probs.shape[1],), dtype=np.float32)
    if strategy == "max":
        return per_window_probs.max(axis=0)
    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        # convert to logits via log, mean, then softmax across classes
        eps = 1e-12
        logits = np.log(np.clip(per_window_probs, eps, 1.0)).mean(axis=0)
        ex = np.exp(logits - logits.max())
        return ex / ex.sum()
    # default: mean
    return per_window_probs.mean(axis=0)

def species_presence(per_window_probs: np.ndarray, classes: List[str], threshold: float, min_windows: int) -> List[str]:
    """Multi-species heuristic: a species is 'present' if its prob >= threshold in >= min_windows windows."""
    if per_window_probs.size == 0:
        return []
    hits = []
    W, C = per_window_probs.shape
    for c in range(C):
        n = int((per_window_probs[:, c] >= threshold).sum())
        if n >= min_windows:
            hits.append(classes[c])
    return hits

def load_whitelist(path: Optional[str]) -> Optional[set]:
    if not path:
        return None
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        print(f"⚠️  Whitelist not found: {path}")
        return None
    keep = set()
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for ln in f:
            name = ln.strip()
            if name:
                keep.add(name)
    return keep

def load_alert_list(path: Optional[str]) -> Optional[set]:
    return load_whitelist(path)

# ---------------- Single-file inference (with optional 2-pass) ----------------
def infer_file(
    wav_path: str,
    model, classes: List[str],
    frames: int, device,
    multi: int, strategy: str, focus: str, focus_k: int,
    processed_root: Optional[str],
    mfcc_stats_path: Optional[str],
    normalize_clip: bool,
    first_pass_whitelist: Optional[set],
    uncertain_threshold: Optional[float],
    presence_threshold: float,
    min_windows: int,
    second_pass: bool,
) -> Dict:
    # ---- Load MFCC features ----
    feature_src = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"
    duration_sec = None
    npy = None
    if processed_root:
        npy = try_find_processed_npy(wav_path, processed_root)
    if npy and os.path.exists(npy):
        a = np.load(npy).astype(np.float32)
        if a.ndim == 3: a = a.squeeze(-1)
        if a.shape[0] != 13:
            raise ValueError(f"Processed file has unexpected shape: {a.shape}")
        mfcc = a
        feature_src = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
    else:
        mfcc, duration_sec = librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160)
        if normalize_clip:
            mfcc = (mfcc - mfcc.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)) / (mfcc.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-6)
        mean, std = load_mfcc_stats(mfcc_stats_path)
        if mean is not None and std is not None:
            mfcc = (mfcc - mean[:, None]) / (std[:, None] + 1e-6)

    # ---- Build windows ----
    windows = build_windows(mfcc, frames, multi)
    if focus == "loudest" and len(windows) > 1:
        scores = np.array([window_rms(w) for w in windows])
        keep_idx = np.argsort(scores)[-min(focus_k, len(windows)):]
        windows = [windows[i] for i in keep_idx]

    # ---- Per-window probs + aggregation ----
    perwin_probs = model_probs_for_windows(model, device, [w[None, ...] for w in windows])  # windows already (13,T); add channel in batching
    # NOTE: model_probs_for_windows expects (W,1,13,T); we passed (W,1,13,T) via [w[None,...]]

    agg_probs = aggregate_probs(perwin_probs, strategy)
    # First pass: apply whitelist mask (if provided)
    agg_probs_pass1 = apply_whitelist(agg_probs, classes, first_pass_whitelist)
    top1_idx = int(np.argmax(agg_probs_pass1)) if agg_probs_pass1.size else -1
    top1_prob = float(agg_probs_pass1[top1_idx]) if top1_idx >= 0 else 0.0
    uncertain = False
    if uncertain_threshold is not None and (top1_prob*100.0) < uncertain_threshold:
        uncertain = True

    used_second_pass = False
    agg_probs_final = agg_probs_pass1.copy()
    perwin_probs_final = perwin_probs.copy()
    if uncertain and second_pass:
        used_second_pass = True
        agg_probs_final = agg_probs  # full class space (no whitelist)
        perwin_probs_final = perwin_probs

    # ---- Multi-species presence heuristic ----
    present = species_presence(perwin_probs_final, classes, threshold=presence_threshold, min_windows=min_windows)

    # ---- Build output dict ----
    res = {
        "file": wav_path,
        "duration_sec": duration_sec,
        "feature_src": feature_src,
        "topk": topk_from_probs(agg_probs_final, classes, k=5),
        "top1_name": classes[int(np.argmax(agg_probs_final))] if agg_probs_final.size else "",
        "top1_prob": float(np.max(agg_probs_final)) if agg_probs_final.size else 0.0,
        "uncertain": uncertain,
        "second_pass_used": used_second_pass,
        "present_species": present,
    }
    return res

# ---------------- Main ----------------
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Batch frog audio inference (multi-file, multi-species, 2-pass).")
    ap.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Folder (recursively scans *.wav) or a single .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--output", default="frog_batch_results.csv", help="Output CSV path")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","frog_cnn_best.pt"))
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","label_map.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=60)
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean","max","logit_mean","logit-mean"], default="logit_mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none","loudest"], default="loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=15)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","data","processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject","models","mfcc_stats.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--normalize", action="store_true", help="Also z-normalize each clip (fallback path)")
    ap.add_argument("--whitelist", help="Optional species whitelist txt (one name per line)")
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=70.0, help="Percent; below => UNCERTAIN")
    ap.add_argument("--second-pass", action="store_true", help="If UNCERTAIN after whitelist, re-evaluate with full class space")
    ap.add_argument("--presence-threshold", type=float, default=0.30, help="Per-window prob threshold for presence")
    ap.add_argument("--min-windows", type=int, default=3, help="Windows required above presence threshold")
    ap.add_argument("--alert-list", help="Optional alert list txt; flag if any appear (in top5 or presence)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    print("=== Batch Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"Input:    {args.input}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Frames:   {args.frames}  | Device: {args.device}")
    print(f"Windows:  {args.multi}  | Strategy: {args.strategy}  | Focus: {args.focus} ({args.focus_k})")
    if args.whitelist: print(f"Whitelist: {args.whitelist}")
    if args.alert_list: print(f"Alert list: {args.alert_list}")
    print(f"Uncertain threshold: {args.uncertain_threshold}%  |  Presence: >= {args.presence_threshold} in >= {args.min_windows} windows")
    print()

    # ---- Load checkpoint & classes ----
    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    target_frames = args.frames
    state_dict = None
    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if "classes" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["classes"], list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]
            print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from checkpoint.")
        if "frames" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["frames"], int):
            target_frames = ckpt["frames"]
            print(f"Using target frames from checkpoint: {target_frames}")
        if "model_state" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["model_state"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["model_state"]
        elif "state_dict" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["state_dict"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["state_dict"]
    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args.label_map)
        print(f"Loaded {len(classes)} classes from label_map.json")

    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=len(classes))
    if state_dict is None:
        if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
            tensor_only = {k: v for k, v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensor_only if tensor_only else ckpt
        else:
            state_dict = ckpt
    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    if missing or unexpected:
        print(f"⚠️  load_state_dict warnings — missing: {list(missing)}, unexpected: {list(unexpected)}")
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    model.to(device).eval()

    whitelist = load_whitelist(args.whitelist)
    alert_set = load_alert_list(args.alert_list)

    # ---- Collect input files ----
    files = []
    if os.path.isdir(args.input):
        for root, _, fnames in os.walk(args.input):
            for fn in fnames:
                if fn.lower().endswith(".wav"):
                    files.append(os.path.join(root, fn))
    else:
        if args.input.lower().endswith(".wav"):
            files.append(args.input)
    files.sort()
    if not files:
        print("No .wav files found.")
        sys.exit(0)

    # ---- Process ----
    out_rows = []
    for idx, fp in enumerate(files, 1):
        print(f"[{idx}/{len(files)}] {fp}")
        try:
            res = infer_file(
                wav_path=fp,
                model=model, classes=classes,
                frames=target_frames, device=device,
                multi=args.multi, strategy=args.strategy, focus=args.focus, focus_k=args.focus_k,
                processed_root=args.processed_root,
                mfcc_stats_path=args.mfcc_stats,
                normalize_clip=args.normalize,
                first_pass_whitelist=whitelist,
                uncertain_threshold=args.uncertain_threshold,
                presence_threshold=args.presence_threshold,
                min_windows=args.min_windows,
                second_pass=args.second_pass,
            )
            # Alerts?
            alert_hits = []
            if alert_set:
                names_to_check = set([n for n,_ in res["topk"]]) | set(res["present_species"])
                for n in names_to_check:
                    if n in alert_set:
                        alert_hits.append(n)

            out_rows.append({
                "file": res["file"],
                "duration_sec": "" if res["duration_sec"] is None else f"{res['duration_sec']:.2f}",
                "feature_src": res["feature_src"],
                "top1_name": res["top1_name"],
                "top1_prob": f"{res['top1_prob']*100:.2f}",
                "top5": json.dumps([{"name": n, "prob_pct": round(p*100, 2)} for n,p in res["topk"]]),
                "uncertain": "yes" if res["uncertain"] else "no",
                "second_pass_used": "yes" if res["second_pass_used"] else "no",
                "present_species": json.dumps(res["present_species"]),
                "alert": "yes" if alert_hits else "no",
                "alert_hits": json.dumps(alert_hits),
            })
        except Exception as e:
            out_rows.append({
                "file": fp, "duration_sec": "", "feature_src": "",
                "top1_name": "", "top1_prob": "", "top5": "[]",
                "uncertain": "", "second_pass_used": "",
                "present_species": "[]", "alert": "", "alert_hits": "[]",
            })
            print(f"  ✖ Error: {e}")

    # ---- Write CSV ----
    fieldnames = ["file","duration_sec","feature_src","top1_name","top1_prob","top5",
                  "uncertain","second_pass_used","present_species","alert","alert_hits"]
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(args.output)), exist_ok=True)
    with open(args.output, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fieldnames)
        w.writeheader()
        for r in out_rows: w.writerow(r)

    print(f"\n✅ Saved: {args.output}")
    print("Done.")
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

How to run (Oakville field setup)

Put your Oakville whitelist here (from my previous message):
FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist_oakville.txt

Run batch inference on a folder of WAVs (recurses into subfolders):

# Adjust this to your folder of recordings (can be your SD card dump)
$in = "C:\Users\dhruv\Recordings"

python FrogSoundProject\src\batch_infer.py `
  --input "$in" `
  --output "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv" `
  --model "FrogSoundProject\models\frog_cnn_best.pt" `
  --label-map "FrogSoundProject\models\label_map.json" `
  --frames 100 `
  --multi 60 `
  --strategy logit_mean `
  --focus loudest `
  --focus-k 15 `
  --processed-root "FrogSoundProject\data\processed" `
  --mfcc-stats "FrogSoundProject\models\mfcc_stats.json" `
  --whitelist "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist_oakville.txt" `
  --uncertain-threshold 70 `
  --second-pass `
  --presence-threshold 0.30 `
  --min-windows 3


Whitelist + 2-pass: it will try local species first; if the top probability is below 70%, it automatically second-passes with the full class set (helpful to catch potential invasives included in your 65-class model).

Multi-species: if any species exceeds 30% in ≥3 windows, it’s listed in present_species (you can tweak --presence-threshold/--min-windows).

Output: oakville_results.csv includes top1, top5, uncertain, present_species, and alert columns.

Optional: invasive alert list

Create e.g. FrogSoundProject\data\invasive_watchlist.txt:

eleutherodactylus_coqui
osteopilus_septentrionalis
eleutherodactylus_planirostris


Then add:

  --alert-list "FrogSoundProject\data\invasive_watchlist.txt"


Any hits in top-5 or presence are flagged.

Field tips (quick)

Recording: 44.1k/48k mono is fine; avoid auto-gain; aim mic toward callers; 5–20 s per clip.

Batching: Dump SD card → single parent folder → run the command above.

Uncertainty: Keep --uncertain-threshold high (e.g., 70–80). Investigate all “UNCERTAIN” or “ALERT: yes”.

Locality: Swap the whitelist when surveying a new region; keep 2-pass enabled for possible non-locals.

If you want, I can also give you a one-click .bat wrapper that sets the arguments and drops the CSV into a dated folder.

You’re super close—two things went wrong in that run:

the whitelist + alert-list files didn’t exist (hence the warnings), and

the input folder you pointed to didn’t have any .wav files (or wasn’t the right folder).

Here’s a clean, copy-paste set of commands that fixes both and gets you a real batch run.

1) Create the Oakville whitelist + an “invasive/watch” list

These names are limited to species that actually exist in your 65-class model and plausibly occur in Oakville/Halton (or are good local proxies). This helps the model ignore far-away species.

# Oakville whitelist (strict, natives only)
Set-Content -Path "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist_oakville.txt" -Value @"
anaxyrus_americanus
hyla_versicolor
lithobates_catesbeianus
lithobates_clamitans
pseudacris_crucifer
"@

# Optional: invasive / watchlist (flag if seen)
Set-Content -Path "FrogSoundProject\data\invasive_watchlist.txt" -Value @"
eleutherodactylus_coqui
osteopilus_septentrionalis
hyla_cinerea
eleutherodactylus_planirostris
gastrophryne_olivacea
"@


Note: I intentionally did not include acris_blanchardi or gastrophryne_olivacea in the Oakville whitelist—those aren’t local; that’s why your earlier mispredictions disappeared when you used a whitelist.

2) Point --input at a folder that actually has WAVs

Quick sanity check (replace the path with your real folder):

You’ll get a CSV at FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv with:

(Small patch) One-line fix for a tiny batching detail

In FrogSoundProject/src/batch_infer.py, find the line inside infer_file that says:

perwin_probs = model_probs_for_windows(model, device, [w[None, ...] for w in windows])


and replace it with:

perwin_probs = model_probs_for_windows(model, device, windows)


(The function already adds the channel dimension; passing w[None,...] double-adds it. If you copied my latest script, this may already be correct.)

ChatGPT said:
Thought for 43s

You hit a simple shape bug in batch_infer.py: each window was being expanded twice, so the tensor going into Conv2d had shape [1, 1, 1, 13, 100] instead of [N, 1, 13, 100].

Below is a drop-in fixed batch_infer.py that standardizes shapes:

Windows are np arrays of shape (13, T) (no extra dims).

They’re stacked once to (N, 1, 13, T) right before the model.

I also kept your whitelist, alert-list, presence detection, second pass, and multi-window options.

Replace your file with this version, then re-run the exact command you used.

FrogSoundProject/src/batch_infer.py (full fixed script)
import os, sys, csv, json, glob, argparse, numpy as np
import librosa
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional

# ---------------- Model (matches infer_wav.py) ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64 * 13 * 1 = 832
        )
        # linear layers at indices 2 and 5 to match your checkpoint
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),                 # 0
            nn.Identity(),                # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256),      # 2
            nn.ReLU(),                    # 3
            nn.Identity(),                # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes),    # 5
        )
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Utils ----------------
def pad_or_crop(a: np.ndarray, T: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """a: (13, t) -> (13, T)"""
    c, t = a.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
        a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
    elif t > T:
        a = a[:, :T]
    return a

def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path: str, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160) -> np.ndarray:
    y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13, n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, center=True, norm="ortho")
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)  # (13, T)

def try_find_processed_npy(wav_path: str, processed_root: str) -> Optional[str]:
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0]
    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(processed_root, "**", f"{stem}*.npy"), recursive=True)
    return hits[0] if hits else None

def load_mfcc_stats(stats_path: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Optional[np.ndarray], Optional[np.ndarray]]:
    if stats_path and os.path.exists(stats_path):
        with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            js = json.load(f)
        mean = np.asarray(js.get("mean"), dtype=np.float32)
        std  = np.asarray(js.get("std"),  dtype=np.float32)
        if mean.shape == (13,) and std.shape == (13,) and np.all(std > 0):
            return mean, std
    return None, None

def load_label_map_any(path: Optional[str], fallback=None) -> List[str]:
    cls = None
    if path and os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lm = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
            cls = lm
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm and isinstance(lm["classes"], list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = lm["classes"]
            elif all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = [x["name"] for x in lm["classes"]]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k, str) for k in lm.keys()) and all(isinstance(v, int) for v in lm.values()):
            size = max(lm.values()) + 1
            cls = [None] * size
            for name, idx in lm.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size:
                    cls[idx] = name
            if any(x is None for x in cls):
                cls = [k for k, _ in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in lm.values()):
            try:
                kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm.items()]
                kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                cls = [v for _, v in kv]
            except Exception:
                pass
    return cls if cls is not None else (fallback or [])

def window_rms(mfcc_win: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mfcc_win ** 2)))

def build_windows(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int, multi: int) -> List[np.ndarray]:
    """Return list of (13, T) windows."""
    total = mfcc.shape[1]
    if total <= T or multi <= 1:
        return [pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)]
    starts = np.linspace(0, max(total - T, 0), num=multi, dtype=int)
    return [pad_or_crop(mfcc[:, s:s+T], T) for s in starts]

def model_probs_for_windows(model: nn.Module, device: torch.device, windows: List[np.ndarray]) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    windows: list of (13, T) np arrays
    returns: (N, C) softmax probabilities
    """
    if len(windows) == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float32)
    x = torch.from_numpy(np.stack(windows))               # (N, 13, T)
    x = x.unsqueeze(1).to(device)                         # (N, 1, 13, T)
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits = model(x)                                 # (N, C)
        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()    # (N, C)
    return probs

def aggregate_probs(perwin_probs: np.ndarray, strategy: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    perwin_probs: (N, C) softmax
    strategy: mean|max|logit_mean
    returns: (C,)
    """
    if perwin_probs.ndim != 2:
        return perwin_probs
    if strategy == "max":
        return perwin_probs.max(axis=0)
    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        # convert to logits safely
        eps = 1e-8
        logits = np.log(np.clip(perwin_probs, eps, 1.0))  # unnormalized ok for relative weighting
        mean_logits = logits.mean(axis=0)
        exps = np.exp(mean_logits - mean_logits.max())
        return exps / exps.sum()
    # default mean
    return perwin_probs.mean(axis=0)

def apply_whitelist_mask(classes: List[str], whitelist_path: Optional[str]) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Return a boolean mask of size C with True for allowed classes, or None if no whitelist."""
    if not whitelist_path or not os.path.exists(whitelist_path):
        return None
    with open(whitelist_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        allowed = {ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()}
    mask = np.array([c in allowed for c in classes], dtype=bool)
    return mask

def apply_mask_and_renorm(probs: np.ndarray, mask: Optional[np.ndarray]) -> np.ndarray:
    if mask is None:
        return probs
    p = probs.copy()
    p[~mask] = 0.0
    s = p.sum()
    return p if s <= 0 else p / s

def species_presence_from_windows(perwin_probs: np.ndarray, classes: List[str], threshold: float, min_windows: int, mask: Optional[np.ndarray]) -> List[str]:
    """
    perwin_probs: (N, C)
    Return list of species present in >= min_windows with per-window prob >= threshold,
    filtered by whitelist mask if provided.
    """
    if perwin_probs.size == 0:
        return []
    N, C = perwin_probs.shape
    counts = (perwin_probs >= threshold).sum(axis=0)  # (C,)
    present_idx = np.where(counts >= min_windows)[0]
    if mask is not None:
        present_idx = [i for i in present_idx if mask[i]]
    return [classes[i] for i in present_idx]

# ---------------- Batch inference ----------------
def infer_file(
    wav_path: str,
    model: nn.Module,
    device: torch.device,
    classes: List[str],
    frames: int,
    multi: int,
    strategy: str,
    focus: str,
    focus_k: int,
    processed_root: Optional[str],
    mfcc_stats_path: Optional[str],
) -> Tuple[Optional[np.ndarray], Optional[np.ndarray], str]:
    """
    Returns (agg_probs (C,), perwin_probs (N,C), feature_source)
    On failure: (None, None, reason)
    """
    # Prefer processed MFCC if available
    feature_src = ""
    mfcc = None
    if processed_root:
        npy = try_find_processed_npy(wav_path, processed_root)
        if npy and os.path.exists(npy):
            a = np.load(npy).astype(np.float32)
            if a.ndim == 3:
                a = a.squeeze(-1)
            if a.shape[0] == 13:
                mfcc = a
                feature_src = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
    if mfcc is None:
        # fallback librosa
        mfcc = librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160)
        feature_src = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"
        # optional global normalization
        mean, std = load_mfcc_stats(mfcc_stats_path)
        if mean is not None and std is not None:
            mfcc = (mfcc - mean[:, None]) / (std[:, None] + 1e-6)

    # Build windows (each (13, T))
    windows = build_windows(mfcc, frames, multi)

    # Optional focus on loudest windows
    if focus == "loudest" and len(windows) > 0:
        scores = [window_rms(w) for w in windows]
        keep_idx = np.argsort(scores)[-min(focus_k, len(windows)):]
        windows = [windows[i] for i in keep_idx]

    if len(windows) == 0:
        return None, None, "no_windows"

    # Per-window probs (N, C)
    perwin_probs = model_probs_for_windows(model, device, windows)

    # Aggregate across windows -> (C,)
    agg = aggregate_probs(perwin_probs, strategy)

    return agg, perwin_probs, feature_src

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Batch frog inference over a folder of WAVs.")
    ap.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Folder or a single .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="CSV path")
    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=60)
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean", "max", "logit_mean", "logit-mean"], default="logit_mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none", "loudest"], default="loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=15)
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "data", "processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "models", "mfcc_stats.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--whitelist", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=70.0)
    ap.add_argument("--second-pass", action="store_true", help="Enable presence detection across windows")
    ap.add_argument("--presence-threshold", type=float, default=0.30, help="Per-window prob threshold for presence")
    ap.add_argument("--min-windows", type=int, default=3, help="#windows above threshold to mark presence")
    ap.add_argument("--alert-list", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    # Load checkpoint + classes
    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    frames = args.frames
    state_dict = None
    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if "classes" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["classes"], list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]
        if "frames" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["frames"], int):
            frames = ckpt["frames"]
        if "model_state" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["model_state"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["model_state"]
        elif "state_dict" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["state_dict"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["state_dict"]
    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args["label_map"])
    n_classes = len(classes)

    # Build & load model
    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=n_classes)
    if state_dict is None:
        if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
            tensor_only = {k: v for k, v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensor_only if tensor_only else ckpt
        else:
            state_dict = ckpt
    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    model.to(device)
    model.eval()

    # Whitelist / alert list
    wl_mask = apply_whitelist_mask(classes, args.whitelist)
    if args.whitelist and wl_mask is None:
        print(f"⚠️  Whitelist not found: {args.whitelist}")
    alert_set = set()
    if args.alert_list and os.path.exists(args.alert_list):
        with open(args.alert_list, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            alert_set = {ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()}
    elif args.alert_list:
        print(f"⚠️  Whitelist not found: {args.alert_list}")

    # Collect WAVs
    inputs = []
    if os.path.isdir(args.input):
        inputs = [str(p) for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(args.input, "**", "*.wav"), recursive=True)]
    elif args.input.lower().endswith(".wav"):
        inputs = [args.input]
    else:
        print("No .wav files found.")
        with open(args.output, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            csv.writer(f).writerow(["path", "error"])
        return

    print("=== Batch Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"Input:    {args.input}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Frames:   {frames}  | Device: {args.device}")
    print(f"Windows:  {args.multi}  | Strategy: {args.strategy}  | Focus: {args.focus} ({args.focus_k})")
    print(f"Whitelist: {args.whitelist}")
    print(f"Alert list: {args.alert_list}")
    print(f"Uncertain threshold: {args.uncertain_threshold:.1f}%  |  Presence: >= {args.presence_threshold} in >= {args.min_windows} windows\n")

    # CSV header
    fields = [
        "path", "feature_src",
        "top1_name", "top1_prob",
        "top5", "uncertain",
        "present_species", "alert", "alert_hits",
        "error"
    ]
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output), exist_ok=True)

    with open(args.output, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f_out:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f_out, fieldnames=fields)
        writer.writeheader()

        for idx, wav in enumerate(inputs, 1):
            print(f"[{idx}/{len(inputs)}] {wav}")
            row = {"path": wav, "error": ""}
            try:
                agg_probs, perwin_probs, feature_src = infer_file(
                    wav, model, device, classes, frames,
                    args.multi, args.strategy, args.focus, args.focus_k,
                    args.processed_root, args.mfcc_stats,
                )

                if agg_probs is None:
                    row.update({
                        "feature_src": feature_src,
                        "error": "inference_failed",
                    })
                    print("  ✖ Error: inference_failed")
                    writer.writerow(row)
                    continue

                # Apply whitelist mask
                agg_probs = apply_mask_and_renorm(agg_probs, wl_mask)

                # Top-k summary
                top_idx = np.argsort(-agg_probs)[:5]
                top_names = [classes[i] for i in top_idx]
                top_probs = [float(agg_probs[i]) for i in top_idx]
                row["feature_src"] = feature_src
                row["top1_name"] = top_names[0]
                row["top1_prob"] = f"{top_probs[0]*100:.2f}%"
                row["top5"] = "; ".join(f"{n}:{p*100:.2f}%" for n, p in zip(top_names, top_probs))

                # Uncertain flag
                uncertain = (top_probs[0] * 100.0) < float(args.uncertain_threshold)
                row["uncertain"] = "yes" if uncertain else "no"

                # Second-pass presence detection
                present_list = []
                if args.second_pass and perwin_probs is not None and perwin_probs.size > 0:
                    # Mask per-window too
                    if wl_mask is not None:
                        perwin_probs = perwin_probs * wl_mask[np.newaxis, :]
                        s = perwin_probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
                        nz = (s > 0).astype(np.float32)
                        perwin_probs = np.where(nz, perwin_probs / np.clip(s, 1e-8, None), perwin_probs)
                    present_list = species_presence_from_windows(
                        perwin_probs, classes,
                        threshold=float(args.presence_threshold),
                        min_windows=int(args.min_windows),
                        mask=wl_mask
                    )

                row["present_species"] = "; ".join(present_list)

                # Alerts
                alert_hits = [sp for sp in present_list if sp in alert_set]
                row["alert"] = "yes" if alert_hits else "no"
                row["alert_hits"] = "; ".join(alert_hits)

                writer.writerow(row)

            except Exception as e:
                row["error"] = str(e)
                print(f"  ✖ Error: {e}")
                writer.writerow(row)

    print(f"\n✅ Saved: {args.output}\nDone.")
    return


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Why this fixes your error

Before: some code paths added an extra dimension to each window (e.g., w[None, ...]) and then the helper added another channel dimension, yielding (1, 1, 1, 13, 100).

Now: windows are always (13, T), we stack them to (N, 13, T), and add one channel to get (N, 1, 13, T) as expected by Conv2d.

Re-run (same command is fine)

Use the exact command you ran previously (no changes needed):

  --output "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv" `

  --label-map "FrogSoundProject\models\label_map.json" `
  --frames 100 `
  --multi 60 `

  --focus loudest `
  --focus-k 15 `
  --processed-root "FrogSoundProject\data\processed" `
  --mfcc-stats "FrogSoundProject\models\mfcc_stats.json" `
  --whitelist "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist_oakville.txt" `
  --uncertain-threshold 70 `
  --second-pass `
  --presence-threshold 0.30 `
  --min-windows 3 `
  --alert-list "FrogSoundProject\data\invasive_watchlist.txt"

Here’s what to do next (and a couple quick tips):

1) Open and skim results

Open in Excel:
Invoke-Item "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv"

Show only alerts (invasives flagged):

Import-Csv "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv" |
  Where-Object { $_.alert -eq 'yes' } |
  Select-Object path, alert_hits, present_species, top5


Uncertain files (top1 < your threshold):

Import-Csv "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv" |
  Where-Object { $_.uncertain -eq 'yes' } |
  Select-Object path, top1_name, top1_prob


Quick species counts (by top1):

Import-Csv "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv" |
  Group-Object top1_name | Sort-Object Count -Desc |
  Select-Object Name, Count


Presence-based counts (uses the per-window rule):

$rows = Import-Csv "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv"
$rows | ForEach-Object {
  ($_.present_species -split '; ') | Where-Object { $_ } |
    ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{ species = $_ } }
} | Group-Object species | Sort-Object Count -Desc | Select Name, Count

2) Run on your actual recordings folder

Your last run processed our sample dataset path. To run on your recorder/SD-card dump, point --input to that folder (or set $in first), e.g.

$in = "C:\Users\dhruv\Recordings"   # <- your real folder
python FrogSoundProject\src\batch_infer.py `
  --input "$in" `
  --output "FrogSoundProject\reports\oakville_results.csv" `
  --model "FrogSoundProject\models\frog_cnn_best.pt" `
  --label-map "FrogSoundProject\models\label_map.json" `
  --frames 100 `
  --multi 60 `
  --strategy logit_mean `
  --focus loudest `
  --focus-k 15 `
  --processed-root "FrogSoundProject\data\processed" `
  --mfcc-stats "FrogSoundProject\models\mfcc_stats.json" `
  --whitelist "FrogSoundProject\data\species_whitelist_oakville.txt" `
  --uncertain-threshold 70 `
  --second-pass `
  --presence-threshold 0.30 `
  --min-windows 3 `
  --alert-list "FrogSoundProject\data\invasive_watchlist.txt"

3) Tuning for field recordings (Oakville)

If you get too many “UNCERTAIN”, try --presence-threshold 0.25 or --min-windows 2.

To speed up: reduce --multi 60 → --multi 24 (and maybe --focus-k 8).

Keep the Oakville whitelist tight to suppress exotic classes.

The present_species column is your friend when multiple frogs call in one file — it uses the per-window rule and is more robust than “top1”.

If you want, I can add a one-click script to summarize the CSV into a compact report (species tallies, alert hits, and a per-file summary) or wire this into the Streamlit app.

Thought for 29s

“No .wav files found” is almost always one of these:

The folder doesn’t actually have .wav files (maybe they’re .WAV, .m4a, .mp3, etc.).

Files are inside subfolders and the script isn’t finding them.

OneDrive “cloud-only” placeholders aren’t hydrated locally.

You’re in a fresh PowerShell where $in wasn’t set (doesn’t look like it here—you set it).

Here’s how to fix fast:

A) Quick sanity checks (PowerShell)
# See what’s really there (recurses)
Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\dhruv\Recordings" -Recurse -File -Include *.wav,*.WAV,*.mp3,*.m4a,*.flac |
  Select-Object FullName, Length | Format-Table -Auto

# Count them
(Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\dhruv\Recordings" -Recurse -File -Include *.wav,*.WAV,*.mp3,*.m4a,*.flac).Count


If this prints 0, the folder is empty or files are another type/location.

If you see files, they’re just not being picked up by batch_infer.py’s finder.

Also, for OneDrive, right-click the folder → Always keep on this device to hydrate files.

B) Make batch_infer.py find everything (wav/WAV/mp3/m4a/flac, recursively)

Drop this tiny patch into your batch_infer.py (it’s self-contained; you can paste it near the top and call list_audio_files where you currently build your file list). It’s case-insensitive and recursive.

# --- robust file discovery (drop into batch_infer.py) ---
import os

def list_audio_files(input_path, exts=(".wav",".mp3",".m4a",".flac")):
    """Return a list of audio files from a file or directory (recursive), case-insensitive on extensions."""
    # normalize to lower-case set for fast checks
    exts_norm = tuple(e.lower() for e in exts)

    files = []
    if os.path.isfile(input_path):
        if input_path.lower().endswith(exts_norm):
            files.append(os.path.abspath(input_path))
    elif os.path.isdir(input_path):
        for root, _, fnames in os.walk(input_path):
            for fn in fnames:
                if fn.lower().endswith(exts_norm):
                    files.append(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(root, fn)))
    else:
        # not a file or dir – just return empty
        return []

    return sorted(files)

# Example usage inside main():
#   wavs = list_audio_files(args.input, exts=tuple(x.strip().lower() for x in args.include_exts.split(",")))


Add a CLI option so you can customize extensions if you want:

# argparse additions (in your ArgumentParser)
ap.add_argument("--include-exts", default="wav,WAV,mp3,m4a,flac",
                help="Comma-separated extensions to include (case-insensitive)")

# then when building the list:
exts = tuple(s.strip().lower() for s in args.include_exts.split(",") if s.strip())
wavs = list_audio_files(args.input, exts=tuple("." + e.lstrip(".") for e in exts))
if not wavs:
    print("No .wav files found.")  # you can change this to 'No audio files found.'
    return
print(f"Found {len(wavs)} audio files.")


If you don’t want to edit the script right now, you can still confirm files exist with the PowerShell checks above. If they do, doing this small patch will make batch_infer.py pick them up.

  --presence-threshold 0.30 `
  --min-windows 3 `

  --include-exts "wav,WAV,mp3,m4a,flac"

D) If your recorder makes other formats

If it’s AAC in .m4a or MP3, the patch above will include them and librosa will read them (it may use audioread/ffmpeg under the hood).

If it’s something odd (e.g., .W64, .CAF), either add that extension to --include-exts or convert to WAV with ffmpeg:

# example conversion for all non-wav to wav (optional)
Get-ChildItem "C:\Users\dhruv\Recordings" -Recurse -File -Include *.mp3,*.m4a,*.flac |
  ForEach-Object {
    $out = [System.IO.Path]::ChangeExtension($_.FullName, ".wav")
    ffmpeg -y -i "$($_.FullName)" -ar 16000 -ac 1 "$out"
  }


If you try the quick checks and still get stuck, paste the output of the two Get-ChildItem commands and I’ll zero in on it.

import os, sys, csv, json, glob, argparse, numpy as np
import librosa
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional

# ---------------- Model (matches infer_wav.py) ----------------
class FrogCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_classes, in_ch=1):
        super().__init__()
        self.features = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(in_ch, 16, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(), nn.MaxPool2d((1,2)),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 3, padding=1), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool2d((13, 1)),  # -> 64 * 13 * 1 = 832
        )
        # linear layers at indices 2 and 5 to match your checkpoint
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Flatten(),                 # 0
            nn.Identity(),                # 1
            nn.Linear(64*13*1, 256),      # 2
            nn.ReLU(),                    # 3
            nn.Identity(),                # 4
            nn.Linear(256, n_classes),    # 5
        )
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.classifier(self.features(x))

# ---------------- Utils ----------------
def pad_or_crop(a: np.ndarray, T: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """a: (13, t) -> (13, T)"""
    c, t = a.shape
    if t < T:
        pad = np.zeros((c, T - t), dtype=a.dtype)
        a = np.concatenate([a, pad], axis=1)
    elif t > T:
        a = a[:, :T]
    return a

def librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path: str, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160) -> np.ndarray:
    y, sr = librosa.load(wav_path, sr=sr_target, mono=True)
    mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(y=y, sr=sr, n_mfcc=13, n_fft=n_fft, hop_length=hop_length, center=True, norm="ortho")
    return mfcc.astype(np.float32)  # (13, T)

def try_find_processed_npy(wav_path: str, processed_root: str) -> Optional[str]:
    stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(wav_path))[0]
    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(processed_root, "**", f"{stem}*.npy"), recursive=True)
    return hits[0] if hits else None

def load_mfcc_stats(stats_path: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Optional[np.ndarray], Optional[np.ndarray]]:
    if stats_path and os.path.exists(stats_path):
        with open(stats_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            js = json.load(f)
        mean = np.asarray(js.get("mean"), dtype=np.float32)
        std  = np.asarray(js.get("std"),  dtype=np.float32)
        if mean.shape == (13,) and std.shape == (13,) and np.all(std > 0):
            return mean, std
    return None, None

def load_label_map_any(path: Optional[str], fallback=None) -> List[str]:
    cls = None
    if path and os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lm = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(lm, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm):
            cls = lm
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and "classes" in lm and isinstance(lm["classes"], list):
            if all(isinstance(x, str) for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = lm["classes"]
            elif all(isinstance(x, dict) and "name" in x for x in lm["classes"]):
                cls = [x["name"] for x in lm["classes"]]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(k, str) for k in lm.keys()) and all(isinstance(v, int) for v in lm.values()):
            size = max(lm.values()) + 1
            cls = [None] * size
            for name, idx in lm.items():
                if 0 <= idx < size:
                    cls[idx] = name
            if any(x is None for x in cls):
                cls = [k for k, _ in sorted(lm.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])]
        elif isinstance(lm, dict) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in lm.values()):
            try:
                kv = [(int(k), v) for k, v in lm.items()]
                kv.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                cls = [v for _, v in kv]
            except Exception:
                pass
    return cls if cls is not None else (fallback or [])

def window_rms(mfcc_win: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(mfcc_win ** 2)))

def build_windows(mfcc: np.ndarray, T: int, multi: int) -> List[np.ndarray]:
    """Return list of (13, T) windows."""
    total = mfcc.shape[1]
    if total <= T or multi <= 1:
        return [pad_or_crop(mfcc, T)]
    starts = np.linspace(0, max(total - T, 0), num=multi, dtype=int)
    return [pad_or_crop(mfcc[:, s:s+T], T) for s in starts]

def model_probs_for_windows(model: nn.Module, device: torch.device, windows: List[np.ndarray]) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    windows: list of (13, T) np arrays
    returns: (N, C) softmax probabilities
    """
    if len(windows) == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float32)
    x = torch.from_numpy(np.stack(windows))               # (N, 13, T)
    x = x.unsqueeze(1).to(device)                         # (N, 1, 13, T)
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits = model(x)                                 # (N, C)
        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()    # (N, C)
    return probs

def aggregate_probs(perwin_probs: np.ndarray, strategy: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    perwin_probs: (N, C) softmax
    strategy: mean|max|logit_mean
    returns: (C,)
    """
    if perwin_probs.ndim != 2:
        return perwin_probs
    if strategy == "max":
        return perwin_probs.max(axis=0)
    if strategy in ("logit_mean", "logit-mean"):
        # convert to logits safely
        eps = 1e-8
        logits = np.log(np.clip(perwin_probs, eps, 1.0))  # unnormalized ok for relative weighting
        mean_logits = logits.mean(axis=0)
        exps = np.exp(mean_logits - mean_logits.max())
        return exps / exps.sum()
    # default mean
    return perwin_probs.mean(axis=0)

def apply_whitelist_mask(classes: List[str], whitelist_path: Optional[str]) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Return a boolean mask of size C with True for allowed classes, or None if no whitelist."""
    if not whitelist_path or not os.path.exists(whitelist_path):
        return None
    with open(whitelist_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        allowed = {ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()}
    mask = np.array([c in allowed for c in classes], dtype=bool)
    return mask

def apply_mask_and_renorm(probs: np.ndarray, mask: Optional[np.ndarray]) -> np.ndarray:
    if mask is None:
        return probs
    p = probs.copy()
    p[~mask] = 0.0
    s = p.sum()
    return p if s <= 0 else p / s

def species_presence_from_windows(perwin_probs: np.ndarray, classes: List[str], threshold: float, min_windows: int, mask: Optional[np.ndarray]) -> List[str]:
    """
    perwin_probs: (N, C)
    Return list of species present in >= min_windows with per-window prob >= threshold,
    filtered by whitelist mask if provided.
    """
    if perwin_probs.size == 0:
        return []
    N, C = perwin_probs.shape
    counts = (perwin_probs >= threshold).sum(axis=0)  # (C,)
    present_idx = np.where(counts >= min_windows)[0]
    if mask is not None:
        present_idx = [i for i in present_idx if mask[i]]
    return [classes[i] for i in present_idx]

# ---------------- Batch inference ----------------
def infer_file(
    wav_path: str,
    model: nn.Module,
    device: torch.device,
    classes: List[str],
    frames: int,
    multi: int,
    strategy: str,
    focus: str,
    focus_k: int,
    processed_root: Optional[str],
    mfcc_stats_path: Optional[str],
) -> Tuple[Optional[np.ndarray], Optional[np.ndarray], str]:
    """
    Returns (agg_probs (C,), perwin_probs (N,C), feature_source)
    On failure: (None, None, reason)
    """
    # Prefer processed MFCC if available
    feature_src = ""
    mfcc = None
    if processed_root:
        npy = try_find_processed_npy(wav_path, processed_root)
        if npy and os.path.exists(npy):
            a = np.load(npy).astype(np.float32)
            if a.ndim == 3:
                a = a.squeeze(-1)
            if a.shape[0] == 13:
                mfcc = a
                feature_src = "Processed (.npy, auto)"
    if mfcc is None:
        # fallback librosa
        mfcc = librosa_mfcc_13(wav_path, sr_target=16000, n_fft=1024, hop_length=160)
        feature_src = "Librosa MFCC (fallback)"
        # optional global normalization
        mean, std = load_mfcc_stats(mfcc_stats_path)
        if mean is not None and std is not None:
            mfcc = (mfcc - mean[:, None]) / (std[:, None] + 1e-6)

    # Build windows (each (13, T))
    windows = build_windows(mfcc, frames, multi)

    # Optional focus on loudest windows
    if focus == "loudest" and len(windows) > 0:
        scores = [window_rms(w) for w in windows]
        keep_idx = np.argsort(scores)[-min(focus_k, len(windows)):]
        windows = [windows[i] for i in keep_idx]

    if len(windows) == 0:
        return None, None, "no_windows"

    # Per-window probs (N, C)
    perwin_probs = model_probs_for_windows(model, device, windows)

    # Aggregate across windows -> (C,)
    agg = aggregate_probs(perwin_probs, strategy)

    return agg, perwin_probs, feature_src

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Batch frog inference over a folder of WAVs.")
    ap.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Folder or a single .wav")
    ap.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="CSV path")
    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--label-map", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--frames", type=int, default=100)
    ap.add_argument("--multi", type=int, default=60)
    ap.add_argument("--strategy", choices=["mean", "max", "logit_mean", "logit-mean"], default="logit_mean")
    ap.add_argument("--focus", choices=["none", "loudest"], default="loudest")
    ap.add_argument("--focus-k", type=int, default=15)
    ap.add_argument("--processed-root", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "data", "processed"))
    ap.add_argument("--mfcc-stats", default=os.path.join("FrogSoundProject", "models", "mfcc_stats.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--whitelist", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--uncertain-threshold", type=float, default=70.0)
    ap.add_argument("--second-pass", action="store_true", help="Enable presence detection across windows")
    ap.add_argument("--presence-threshold", type=float, default=0.30, help="Per-window prob threshold for presence")
    ap.add_argument("--min-windows", type=int, default=3, help="#windows above threshold to mark presence")
    ap.add_argument("--alert-list", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    # Load checkpoint + classes
    ckpt = torch.load(args.model, map_location="cpu")
    classes = None
    frames = args.frames
    state_dict = None
    if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
        if "classes" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["classes"], list):
            classes = ckpt["classes"]
        if "frames" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["frames"], int):
            frames = ckpt["frames"]
        if "model_state" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["model_state"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["model_state"]
        elif "state_dict" in ckpt and isinstance(ckpt["state_dict"], dict):
            state_dict = ckpt["state_dict"]
    if classes is None:
        classes = load_label_map_any(args["label_map"])
    n_classes = len(classes)

    # Build & load model
    model = FrogCNN(n_classes=n_classes)
    if state_dict is None:
        if isinstance(ckpt, dict):
            tensor_only = {k: v for k, v in ckpt.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
            state_dict = tensor_only if tensor_only else ckpt
        else:
            state_dict = ckpt
    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=False)
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    model.to(device)
    model.eval()

    # Whitelist / alert list
    wl_mask = apply_whitelist_mask(classes, args.whitelist)
    if args.whitelist and wl_mask is None:
        print(f"⚠️  Whitelist not found: {args.whitelist}")
    alert_set = set()
    if args.alert_list and os.path.exists(args.alert_list):
        with open(args.alert_list, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            alert_set = {ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()}
    elif args.alert_list:
        print(f"⚠️  Whitelist not found: {args.alert_list}")

    # Collect WAVs
    inputs = []
    if os.path.isdir(args.input):
        inputs = [str(p) for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(args.input, "**", "*.wav"), recursive=True)]
    elif args.input.lower().endswith(".wav"):
        inputs = [args.input]
    else:
        print("No .wav files found.")
        with open(args.output, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            csv.writer(f).writerow(["path", "error"])
        return

    print("=== Batch Frog Inference ===")
    print(f"Input:    {args.input}")
    print(f"Model:    {args.model}")
    print(f"Frames:   {frames}  | Device: {args.device}")
    print(f"Windows:  {args.multi}  | Strategy: {args.strategy}  | Focus: {args.focus} ({args.focus_k})")
    print(f"Whitelist: {args.whitelist}")
    print(f"Alert list: {args.alert_list}")
    print(f"Uncertain threshold: {args.uncertain_threshold:.1f}%  |  Presence: >= {args.presence_threshold} in >= {args.min_windows} windows\n")

    # CSV header
    fields = [
        "path", "feature_src",
        "top1_name", "top1_prob",
        "top5", "uncertain",
        "present_species", "alert", "alert_hits",
        "error"
    ]
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output), exist_ok=True)

    with open(args.output, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f_out:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f_out, fieldnames=fields)
        writer.writeheader()

        for idx, wav in enumerate(inputs, 1):
            print(f"[{idx}/{len(inputs)}] {wav}")
            row = {"path": wav, "error": ""}
            try:
                agg_probs, perwin_probs, feature_src = infer_file(
                    wav, model, device, classes, frames,
                    args.multi, args.strategy, args.focus, args.focus_k,
                    args.processed_root, args.mfcc_stats,
                )

                if agg_probs is None:
                    row.update({
                        "feature_src": feature_src,
                        "error": "inference_failed",
                    })
                    print("  ✖ Error: inference_failed")
                    writer.writerow(row)
                    continue

                # Apply whitelist mask
                agg_probs = apply_mask_and_renorm(agg_probs, wl_mask)

                # Top-k summary
                top_idx = np.argsort(-agg_probs)[:5]
                top_names = [classes[i] for i in top_idx]
                top_probs = [float(agg_probs[i]) for i in top_idx]
                row["feature_src"] = feature_src
                row["top1_name"] = top_names[0]
                row["top1_prob"] = f"{top_probs[0]*100:.2f}%"
                row["top5"] = "; ".join(f"{n}:{p*100:.2f}%" for n, p in zip(top_names, top_probs))

                # Uncertain flag
                uncertain = (top_probs[0] * 100.0) < float(args.uncertain_threshold)
                row["uncertain"] = "yes" if uncertain else "no"

                # Second-pass presence detection
                present_list = []
                if args.second_pass and perwin_probs is not None and perwin_probs.size > 0:
                    # Mask per-window too
                    if wl_mask is not None:
                        perwin_probs = perwin_probs * wl_mask[np.newaxis, :]
                        s = perwin_probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
                        nz = (s > 0).astype(np.float32)
                        perwin_probs = np.where(nz, perwin_probs / np.clip(s, 1e-8, None), perwin_probs)
                    present_list = species_presence_from_windows(
                        perwin_probs, classes,
                        threshold=float(args.presence_threshold),
                        min_windows=int(args.min_windows),
                        mask=wl_mask
                    )

                row["present_species"] = "; ".join(present_list)

                # Alerts
                alert_hits = [sp for sp in present_list if sp in alert_set]
                row["alert"] = "yes" if alert_hits else "no"
                row["alert_hits"] = "; ".join(alert_hits)

                writer.writerow(row)

            except Exception as e:
                row["error"] = str(e)
                print(f"  ✖ Error: {e}")
                writer.writerow(row)

    print(f"\n✅ Saved: {args.output}\nDone.")
    return


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
